We evaluate PriorNet on EngageNet, DAiSEE, DREAMS, and PAFE using each dataset's native evaluation protocol. Across these benchmarks, PriorNet improves over the strongest listed prior reference within each dataset's evaluation framing, while component ablations on EngageNet and DAiSEE indicate that the gains arise from complementary contributions of preprocessing, adaptation, and objective-level priors. These results support explicit prior injection as a useful design principle for face-video engagement estimation under the benchmark conditions studied in this work.

</details>


### [72] [The Detector Teaches Itself: Lightweight Self-Supervised Adaptation for Open-Vocabulary Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03642)
*Yazhe Wan,Changjae Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAT是一种自监督微调方法，通过解耦适应训练增强视觉-语言模型对局部区域特征的捕捉能力，从而提升协作式开放词汇目标检测的性能，在COCO和LVIS数据集上达到新最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的协作式开放词汇目标检测方法中，在大规模图文数据上预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLM）擅长处理整图语义，但难以捕捉局部对象的细节特征，导致其在区域级别的检测任务中表现受限。

Method: 提出解耦适应训练（DAT）：(1) 利用预训练的闭集检测器构建区域感知的伪标注数据集，即使新类别对象存在未标注或误标注的情况也能利用；(2) 对VLM的视觉骨干进行解耦微调，通过权重插值在增强局部特征对齐的同时保持全局语义知识；(3) 作为即插即用模块，仅微调<0.8M参数，无需推理开销。

Result: 在COCO和LVIS数据集上，DAT在已知类别和新类别上均持续提升了检测性能，达到了协作式开放词汇检测的最新最优水平。

Conclusion: DAT通过解耦微调策略有效弥合了VLM在全局图像理解与局部区域检测之间的差距，为开放词汇目标检测提供了一种轻量、高效且无需额外推理成本的改进方案。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection aims to recognize objects from an open set of categories, which leverages vision-language models (VLMs) pre-trained on large-scale image-text data. The cooperative paradigm combines an object detector with a VLM to achieve zero-shot recognition of novel objects. However, VLMs pre-trained on full images often struggle to capture local object details, limiting their effectiveness when applied to region-level detection. We present Decoupled Adaptivity Training (DAT), a self-supervised fine-tuning approach to improve VLMs for cooperative model-based object detection. Given a cooperative model consists of a closed-set detector and a VLM, we first construct a region-aware pseudo-labeled dataset using a pre-trained closed-set object detector, in which regions corresponding to novel objects may be present but remain unlabeled or mislabeled. We then fine-tune the visual backbone of the VLM in a decoupled manner, which enhances local feature alignment while preserving global semantic knowledge via weight interpolation. DAT is a plug-and-play module that requires no inference overhead and fine-tunes less than 0.8M parameters. Experiments on the COCO and LVIS datasets show that DAT consistently improves detection performance on both novel and known categories, establishing a new state of the art in cooperative open-vocabulary detection.

</details>


### [73] [AniMatrix: An Anime Video Generation Model that Thinks in Art, Not Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03652)
*Tencent HY Team*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AniMatrix是一个面向动画制作的视频生成模型，通过双通道条件机制和三阶段训练策略（重新定义正确性、覆盖物理先验、区分艺术与失败），解决了现有视频生成模型因物理先验偏差而无法生成符合动画艺术惯例（如拉伸帧、变形、Q版变形）的问题。在专业动画师评估的五个维度中，AniMatrix在四个维度上排名第一，对提示理解（+0.70，+22.4%）和艺术运动（+0.55，+16.9%）的提升最为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型天然内化物理真实性作为先验知识，但动画故意违反物理规律（如动态拉伸、冲击帧、Q版变形等），且数千种并存的动画艺术惯例不存在统一的"动画物理"可供模型学习。物理偏置的模型要么扁平化定义动画媒介的艺术性，要么在风格变异性下崩溃。

Method: 双通道条件机制：Production Knowledge System将动画编码为结构化分类（风格、运动、相机、特效），AniCaption从像素推断这些变量；可训练标签编码器保留分类的字段-值结构，冻结T5编码器处理自由叙事文本，通过交叉注意力（细粒度控制）和AdaLN调制（全局强制）实现双路径注入。三阶段训练：风格-运动-变形课程从近物理运动过渡到完全动画表现力；变形感知偏好优化结合领域特定奖励模型区分有意艺术处理与病理崩溃。

Result: 在专业动画师评估的五个生产维度中，AniMatrix在四个维度上排名第一，最大提升为提示理解（+0.70，+22.4%）和艺术运动（+0.55，+16.9%），优于Seedance-Pro 1.0。

Conclusion: 通过结构化知识编码、课程学习与领域偏好优化，可以有效覆盖视频生成模型的物理先验，实现对动画艺术惯例的忠实生成。代码和模型权重将公开发布。

Abstract: Video generation models internalize physical realism as their prior. Anime deliberately violates physics: smears, impact frames, chibi shifts; and its thousands of coexisting artistic conventions yield no single "physics of anime" a model can absorb. Physics-biased models therefore flatten the artistry that defines the medium or collapse under its stylistic variance. We present AniMatrix, a video generation model that targets artistic rather than physical correctness through a dual-channel conditioning mechanism and a three-step transition: redefine correctness, override the physics prior, and distinguish art from failure. First, a Production Knowledge System encodes anime as a structured taxonomy of controllable production variables (Style, Motion, Camera, VFX), and AniCaption infers these variables from pixels as directorial directives. A trainable tag encoder preserves the field-value structure of this taxonomy while a frozen T5 encoder handles free-form narrative; dual-path injection (cross-attention for fine-grained control, AdaLN modulation for global enforcement) ensures categorical directives are never diluted by open-ended text. Second, a style-motion-deformation curriculum transitions the model from near-physical motion to full anime expressiveness. Third, deformation-aware preference optimization with a domain-specific reward model separates intentional artistry from pathological collapse. On an anime-specific human evaluation with five production dimensions scored by professional animators, AniMatrix ranks first on four of five, with the largest gains over Seedance-Pro 1.0 on Prompt Understanding (+0.70, +22.4 percent) and Artistic Motion (+0.55, +16.9 percent). We will publicly release the AniMatrix model weights and inference code.

</details>


### [74] [Unified Multimodal Visual Tracking with Dual Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03716)
*Lingyi Hong,Jinglun Li,Xinyu Zhou,Kaixun Jiang,Pinxue Guo,Zhaoyu Chen,Runze Li,Xingdong Sheng,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OneTrackerV2提出统一的多模态视觉跟踪框架，通过Meta Merger实现多模态信息嵌入统一空间，并引入Dual Mixture-of-Experts（T-MoE和M-MoE）分别建模时空关系与多模态知识，在5种RGB和RGB+X跟踪任务、12个基准上达到SOTA，同时保持高推理效率，并在模型压缩和模态缺失场景下表现鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对不同模态（如RGB、RGB+X）需分别训练独立模型或依赖预训练模型适配新模态，导致效率低、可扩展性差、实用性受限，缺乏统一的端到端多模态跟踪框架。

Method: 提出OneTrackerV2统一框架，包含：(1) Meta Merger将多模态信息嵌入统一空间，实现灵活融合与鲁棒性；(2) Dual Mixture-of-Experts (DMoE) 包含T-MoE（建模时空关系）和M-MoE（嵌入多模态知识），解耦跨模态依赖并减少特征冲突。采用共享架构、统一参数和单一端到端训练。

Result: 在5种RGB和RGB+X跟踪任务、12个基准上达到SOTA性能，推理效率高。模型压缩后仍保持强性能，且在模态缺失场景下表现出显著鲁棒性。

Conclusion: OneTrackerV2证明了统一参数、共享架构和单一端到端训练的多模态跟踪框架的可行性，兼顾了高效性、可扩展性、鲁棒性与SOTA性能，为多模态视觉跟踪提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal visual object tracking can be divided into to several kinds of tasks (e.g. RGB and RGB+X tracking), based on the input modality. Existing methods often train separate models for each modality or rely on pretrained models to adapt to new modalities, which limits efficiency, scalability, and usability. Thus, we introduce OneTrackerV2, a unified multi-modal tracking framework that enables end-to-end training for any modality. We propose Meta Merger to embed multi-modal information into a unified space, allowing flexible modality fusion and robustness. We further introduce Dual Mixture-of-Experts (DMoE): T-MoE models spatio-temporal relations for tracking, while M-MoE embeds multi-modal knowledge, disentangling cross-modal dependencies and reducing feature conflicts. With a shared architecture, unified parameters, and a single end-to-end training, OneTrackerV2 achieves state-of-the-art performance across five RGB and RGB+X tracking tasks and 12 benchmarks, while maintaining high inference efficiency. Notably, even after model compression, OneTrackerV2 retains strong performance. Moreover, OneTrackerV2 demonstrates remarkable robustness under modality-missing scenarios.

</details>


### [75] [FluxFlow: Conservative Flow-Matching for Astronomical Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03749)
*Shuhong Liu,Xining Ge,Ziteng Cui,Liuzhuozheng Li,Gengjia Chang,Jun Liu,Ziying Gu,Dong Li,Xuangeng Chu,Lin Gu,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FluxFlow是一个结合观测不确定性和源区域重要性权重的像素空间流匹配框架，通过无训练的维纳正则化测试时校正来抑制幻觉源，并在包含19,500对真实地面-空间图像对的DESI-HST数据集上实现了优于现有方法的光度和科学精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地面到空间的天文超分辨率方法存在两个关键问题：(1) 使用合成训练对无法捕捉真实大气统计特性；(2) 要么产生过度平滑的重建结果，要么产生在观测天空中无物理对应物的幻觉源。

Method: 提出FluxFlow框架：(1) 在训练中引入观测不确定性和源区域重要性权重；(2) 使用无训练的维纳正则化测试时校正来抑制幻觉源同时保留恢复的细节；(3) 构建包含19,500对真实共配准地面-空间图像对的DESI-HST数据集，具有真实大气PSF变化。

Result: 在DESI-HST基准数据集上，FluxFlow在光度和科学精度两个指标上均一致优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: FluxFlow通过结合不确定性感知训练和维纳正则化测试时校正，有效解决了地面到空间超分辨率中的幻觉源问题，实现了更可靠的天文图像重建。

Abstract: Ground-to-space astronomical super-resolution requires recovering space-quality images from ground-based observations that are simultaneously limited by pixel sampling resolution and atmospheric seeing, which imposes a stochastic, spatially varying PSF that cannot be resolved through upsampling alone. Existing methods rely on synthetic training pairs that fail to capture real atmospheric statistics and are prone to either over-smoothed reconstructions or hallucination sources with no physical counterpart in the observed sky. We propose FluxFlow, a conservative pixel-space flow-matching framework that incorporates observation uncertainty and source-region importance weights during training, and a training-free Wiener-regularized test-time correction to suppress hallucination sources while preserving recovered detail. We further construct the DESI--HST Dataset, the large-scale real-world benchmark comprising 19,500 real co-registered ground-to-space image pairs with real atmospheric PSF variation. Experiments demonstrate that FluxFlow consistently outperforms existing baseline methods in both photometric and scientific accuracy.

</details>


### [76] [Before Forgetting, Learn to Remember: Revisiting Foundational Learning Failures in LVLM Unlearning Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03759)
*JuneHyoung Kwon,MiHyeon Kim,Eunju Lee,JungMin Yun,Byeonggeuk Lim,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReMem基准，通过解决LVLM中信息记忆不足和多跳诅咒问题，实现可靠的记忆与遗忘评估，并引入曝光度指标量化信息擦除深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘基准使用虚构身份评估LVLM隐私风险，但忽略了模型未能有效记忆目标信息（阶段1失败）的关键问题，导致后续遗忘评估不可靠。

Method: 提出ReMem基准，包含三种策略：1）原则性数据缩放确保基础学习；2）推理感知的QA对；3）多样化视觉上下文。同时提出曝光度（Exposure）指标，从模型内部分布量化信息擦除深度。

Result: 实验表明ReMem为诊断LVLM的学习和遗忘行为提供了严格且可信的框架。

Conclusion: ReMem通过解决记忆不足和多跳诅咒，克服了现有基准的缺陷，为LVLM隐私保护提供了更可靠的评估方法。

Abstract: While Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) offer powerful capabilities, they pose privacy risks by unintentionally memorizing sensitive personal information. Current unlearning benchmarks attempt to mitigate this using fictitious identities but overlook a critical stage 1 failure: models fail to effectively memorize target information initially, rendering subsequent unlearning evaluations unreliable. Diagnosing under-memorization and the multi-hop curse as root causes, we introduce ReMem, a Reliable Multi-hop and Multi-image Memorization Benchmark. ReMem ensures robust foundational learning through principled data scaling, reasoning-aware QA pairs, and diverse visual contexts. Additionally, we propose a novel Exposure metric to quantify the depth of information erasure from the model's internal probability distribution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReMem provides a rigorous and trustworthy framework for diagnosing both learning and unlearning behaviors in LVLMs.

</details>


### [77] [A Deeper Dive into the Irreversibility of PolyProtect: Making Protected Face Templates Harder to Invert](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03857)
*Vedrana Krivokuća Hahn,Jérémy Maceiras,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文深入分析了PolyProtect（一种基于多项式变换的生物特征模板保护方法）的不可逆性，提出了基于余弦距离的数值求解反转方法，并设计了一种"密钥选择算法"来增强模板的不可逆性，同时揭示了嵌入元素范围对精度的影响，并提出了归一化预处理方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PolyProtect是一种将人脸嵌入通过多元多项式转换为保护模板的方法，但其不可逆性（即从保护模板恢复原始嵌入的困难程度）尚未被充分研究。作者希望通过分析其不可逆性，并提出增强不可逆性的方法，以更好地平衡安全性与识别精度之间的权衡。

Method: 作者首先使用基于余弦距离的数值求解器对PolyProtected模板进行逆向攻击（此前工作使用欧氏距离）；然后提出一种"密钥选择算法"来优化多项式的系数和指数，使生成的模板更难被反转；最后通过归一化嵌入元素来改善精度。

Result: 实验表明，基于余弦距离的求解器比欧氏距离更容易反转PolyProtected模板；提出的密钥选择算法能有效生成显著更难反转的模板，并近似均衡不同重叠参数下的不可逆性；嵌入元素的范围会影响精度，归一化预处理可改善精度。

Conclusion: 本文提出的密钥选择算法能有效增强PolyProtect的不可逆性，并更好地控制不可逆性与精度之间的权衡；归一化嵌入可提升保护域内的识别精度。

Abstract: This work presents a deeper analysis of the "irreversibility" property of PolyProtect, a biometric template protection method initially proposed for securing face embeddings. PolyProtect transforms embeddings into protected templates via multivariate polynomials, whose coefficients and exponents are distinct for each subject enrolled in the face recognition system. A polynomial is applied to consecutive sets of elements from a given embedding, where the amount of overlap between the sets is a tunable parameter. We begin our irreversibility analysis by demonstrating that PolyProtected templates are easier to invert using a numerical solver based on cosine distance, as opposed to Euclidean distance (used in the earlier PolyProtect work). To make this inversion more difficult, we then propose a "key selection algorithm", which tries to choose "keys" (coefficients and exponents of the PolyProtect polynomial) that enhance the irreversibility of PolyProtected templates, compared to when the keys are purely random. Our experiments show that this algorithm is effective at generating PolyProtected templates that are significantly more difficult to invert, and that it approximately equalises the irreversibility of PolyProtected templates generated using different "overlap" parameters. This allows for better control of the irreversibility versus accuracy trade-off, known to exist across different overlaps. We also show that accuracy in the PolyProtected domain can be affected by the range in which the embedding elements lie, but that this can be improved by normalizing the embeddings prior to applying PolyProtect. This work is reproducible using our open-source code.

</details>


### [78] [GeoTopoDiff: Learning Geometry--Topology Graph Priors through Boundary-Constrained Mixed Diffusion for Sparse-Slice 3D Porous Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03764)
*Yue Shi,Peng Wang,Mingzhe Yu,Yunlong Zhao,Li Liu,Gareth D Hatton,Yan Lyu,Liangxiu Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoTopoDiff是一个基于图扩散的框架，用于从稀疏CT切片重建3D多孔微观结构。它将扩散先验从体素空间转移到混合图状态空间，同时建模连续孔隙几何和离散孔隙-喉道拓扑，并利用拓扑感知的部分图先验约束反向去噪过程。实验表明，该方法将形态误差降低19.8%，拓扑敏感传输误差降低36.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的体素先验建模在重建大规模3D多孔微观结构时面临挑战，因为需要同时建模连续孔隙形态和离散孔隙-喉道拓扑。现有扩散模型需要完全观测的CT扫描才能提供拓扑保真先验，导致在实际工业应用中吞吐量、拓扑保真度和视野之间存在固有的权衡。

Method: 提出GeoTopoDiff框架，将扩散先验学习从体素空间转移到混合图状态空间，该空间同时包含连续孔隙几何和离散孔隙-喉道拓扑。引入从稀疏CT切片中提取的拓扑感知部分图先验，用于约束反向去噪过程。

Result: 在 anisotropic PTFE 和 Fontainebleau 砂岩上的实验表明，GeoTopoDiff平均降低形态相关误差19.8%，拓扑敏感传输误差36.5%。

Conclusion: 混合图状态空间能够促进扩散去噪过程，在稀疏观测下减少后验不确定性。所有模型和代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion-based voxel prior modelling is challenging for the reconstruction of large-scale 3D porous microstructures. Due to the demanding requirements for simultaneously modelling both the continuous pore morphology and the discrete pore-throat topology, the diffusion models require fully observed CT scans to provide topology-faithful priors, which results in an inherent trade-off among throughput, topological fidelity, and field of view in practical industrial applications. We propose GeoTopoDiff, a graph diffusion-based framework for reconstructing 3D porous microstructures from sparse CT slices. GeoTopoDiff transfers the learning of diffusion priors from a voxel-based space to a mixed graph state space, which simultaneously encompasses continuous pore geometry and discrete pore-throat topology. A topology-aware partial graph prior from sparsely observed CT slices is introduced to constrain the reverse denoising process. Experiments on anisotropic PTFE and Fontainebleau sandstone show that GeoTopoDiff reduces morphology-related errors by 19.8% and topology-sensitive transport errors by 36.5% on average. Our findings suggest that the mixed graph state space promotes the diffusion denoising process to reduce posterior uncertainty under a sparse observations. All models and code have been made publicly available to facilitate the exploration of diffusion models in the field of 3D porous microstructures simulation.

</details>


### [79] [ReLeaf: Benchmarking Leaf Segmentation across Domains and Species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03784)
*Robert Martinko,Daniel Steininger,Julia Simon,Andreas Trondl,Matthias Blaickner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了现有叶片分割数据集和实例分割架构，提出YOLO26模型在精准农业中表现最佳，并发布了包含23个植物物种的新基准数据集，发现跨域泛化性能显著下降，强调了多样化数据集的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球粮食需求增长和气候压力增加了对可持续精准农业的需求，自动化个体植物处理依赖细粒度视觉分析，但叶片级分割因缺乏专用数据集和系统评估而进展缓慢。

Method: 调研现有数据并确定4个可公开获取的叶片分割数据集，对比单阶段、两阶段和基于Transformer的检测器；通过半自动标注CropAndWeed图像创建包含23个植物物种的新基准数据集，并进行跨域泛化实验。

Result: YOLO26模型配置提供最佳实际应用权衡；跨域泛化实验显示植物物种和记录设置间性能显著下降；在4个现有数据集上训练的模型在对应测试集上平均mAP50-95达83.9%，在新基准上达40.2%。

Conclusion: 多样化叶片分割数据集对鲁棒精准农业至关重要，当前模型跨域泛化能力不足，新基准数据集有助于推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Rising global food demand and growing climate pressure increase the need for sustainable, precise agricultural practices. Automated, individualized plant treatment relies on fine-grained visual analysis, yet leaf-level segmentation remains underexplored despite its value for assessing crop health, growth dynamics, yield potential and localized stress symptoms. Progress is limited by a lack of dedicated datasets, especially regarding species coverage, and by the absence of systematic evaluations of modern instance-segmentation architectures for this task. We address these gaps by surveying current data and identifying four suitable, publicly available leaf-segmentation datasets. Using them, we compare one-stage, two-stage and Transformer-based detectors and identify a YOLO26 model configuration to provide the best trade-off for real-world precision-agriculture tasks. Extensive cross-domain generalization experiments reveal substantial performance drops across plant species and recording setups, especially for models trained solely on laboratory data. To strengthen data availability, we introduce a new benchmark dataset with leaf-level masks for 23 plant species, created via semi-automatic annotation of selected CropAndWeed images. A model trained on all four existing datasets achieves a mean mAP50-95 of 83.9% across their corresponding test sets and 40.2% on our new benchmark, demonstrating improved generalization and highlighting the need for diverse leaf-segmentation datasets in robust precision agriculture.

</details>


### [80] [A Robust Unsupervised Domain Adaptation Framework for Medical Image Classification Using RKHS-MMD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03787)
*Sapna Sachan,Rakesh Kumar Sanodiya,Amulya Kumar Mahto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合迁移学习和RKHS最大均值差异（RKHS-MMD）的无监督域适应框架，用于对齐不同医疗中心和成像设备带来的源域与目标域分布差异，在胸部X光数据集上显著提升了未标注数据的分类泛化性能，且比标准MMD更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像标注成本高、依赖领域专家，且不同医疗中心和成像设备引入域偏移与模态差异，导致训练模型泛化能力受限，亟需一种无需目标域人工标注的域适应方法。

Method: 提出无监督域适应框架，结合迁移学习与基于再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）的最大均值差异（MMD）损失，通过联合优化分类损失和RKHS-MMD损失对齐源域与目标域分布。

Result: 在两个不同医疗中心的胸部X光数据集上，该方法相比未经过域适应的模型取得了显著性能提升；对比实验表明RKHS-MMD在减小模态差距方面优于标准MMD。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效减少对人工标注的依赖，提升模型在未标注医学数据集上的泛化能力，RKHS-MMD在医学图像分类中表现出更强的域对齐能力，有望推动AI驱动的医学诊断。

Abstract: Labeling medical images is a major bottleneck in the field of medical imaging, as it requires domain-specific expertise, and it gets further complicated due to variability across different medical centers and different imaging devices. Such heterogeneity introduces domain shifts and modality discrepancies, which limits the generalization of trained models. To address this important challenge, we propose an unsupervised domain adaptation framework that combines transfer learning with a Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space based Maximum Mean Discrepancy loss for the alignment of source and target domains. By jointly optimizing classification and RKHS-MMD losses, the methodology enhances generalization to unannotated medical datasets while diminishing reliance on manual annotation. Experimental evaluations presented on two chest X-ray datasets, which are obtained from different medical centers, show outstanding improvements over models trained without adaptation. Furthermore, we perform a comparative study to see that RKHS-MMD performs better than the standard Maximum Mean Discrepancy in reducing modality gap, emphasizing its effectiveness for medical image classification and also its strong capability in advanced AI-driven medical diagnostics.

</details>


### [81] [Enhancing Visual Question Answering with Multimodal LLMs via Chain-of-Question Guided Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03790)
*Quanxing Xu,Ling Zhou,Xian Zhong,Xiaohua Huang,Rubing Huang,Chia-Wen Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoVQD逻辑提示策略，融合Chain-of-Thought推理与视觉问题分解，并构建CgRAG框架，通过结构化推理引导检索增强生成，从而提升多模态大模型在开放域VQA任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强型VQA方法在多模态大模型与外部知识的整合上仍存在不足，特别是在复杂跨领域场景下，推理与知识获取之间缺乏有效的结构化引导。

Method: 提出CoVQD（Chain-of-Thought + Visual Question Decomposition）逻辑提示策略，将思维链推理与视觉问题分解相融合；并基于此构建CgRAG框架，引导检索获取更准确、连贯的外部知识，辅助MLLM进行推理。

Result: 在E-VQA、InfoSeek和OKVQA三个基准数据集上的大量实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: CoVQD引导的检索增强生成框架能有效提升MLLM在复杂跨领域VQA场景中的泛化能力和可靠性。

Abstract: With advances in multimodal research and deep learning, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for a wide range of multimodal tasks. As a core problem in vision-language research, Visual Question Answering (VQA) has increasingly employed MLLMs to improve performance, particularly in open-domain settings where external knowledge is essential. In this work, we aim to further enhance retrieval-based VQA by more effectively integrating MLLMs with structured reasoning and knowledge acquisition. We introduce a logical prompting strategy that fuses Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning with Visual Question Decomposition (VQD), termed CoVQD, to guide retrieval toward more accurate and relevant knowledge for MLLM inference. Building on this idea, we propose a new framework, CoVQD-guided RAG (CgRAG), which enables MLLMs to access more comprehensive and coherent external knowledge while benefiting from structured visual-text reasoning guidance, thereby improving generalization and reliability in complex cross-domain VQA scenarios. Extensive experiments on E-VQA, InfoSeek, and OKVQA benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [82] [Multimodal Learning on Low-Quality Data with Conformal Predictive Self-Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03820)
*Xun Jiang,Yufan Gu,Disen Hu,Yuqing Hou,Yazhou Yao,Fumin Shen,Heng Tao Shen,Xing Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的框架CPSC（共形预测自校准），利用共形预测解决多模态学习中低质量数据（模态不平衡和噪声污染）问题，通过表示自校准和梯度自校准两大模块，在六个基准数据集上持续优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习中存在两类低质量数据问题——模态不平衡和噪声污染，现有研究通常孤立看待二者。作者认为它们共同源于模型对个体模态和实例可靠性的预测不确定性，亟需一个统一框架加以解决。

Method: 提出CPSC框架，核心是自校准训练循环，包含：(1) 表示自校准：分解单模态特征，通过共形预测器识别并选择性融合最鲁棒的特征分量；(2) 梯度自校准：基于实例级可靠性分数在反向传播中重新校准梯度流，引导优化朝更可信方向进行；(3) 共形预测器的自更新策略，确保系统在训练过程中协同演化。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，分别在模态不平衡和噪声污染两种设置下进行实验，CPSC框架一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: CPSC通过共形预测驱动的自校准机制，统一有效地解决了多模态学习中的模态不平衡和噪声污染问题，验证了利用预测不确定性指导模型自校准的可行性。

Abstract: Multimodal learning often grapples with the challenge of low-quality data, which predominantly manifests as two facets: modality imbalance and noisy corruption. While these issues are often studied in isolation, we argue that they share a common root in the predictive uncertainty towards the reliability of individual modalities and instances during learning. In this paper, we propose a unified framework, termed Conformal Predictive Self-Calibration (CPSC), which leverages conformal prediction to equip the model with the ability to perform self-guided calibration on-the-fly. The core of our proposed CPSC lies in a novel self-calibrating training loop that seamlessly integrates two key modules: (1) Representation Self-Calibration, which decomposes unimodal features into components, and selectively fuses the most robust ones identified by a conformal predictor to enhance feature resilience. (2) Gradient Self-Calibration, which recalibrates the gradient flow during backpropagation based on instance-wise reliability scores, steering the optimization towards more trustworthy directions. Furthermore, we also devise a self-update strategy for the conformal predictor to ensure the entire system co-evolves consistently throughout the training process. Extensive experiments on six benchmark datasets under both imbalanced and noisy settings demonstrate that our CPSC framework consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/XunCHN/CPSC.

</details>


### [83] [Identity-Consistent Multi-Pose Generation of Contactless Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03830)
*Zhiyu Pan,Xiongjun Guan,Jianjiang Feng,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IMPOSE框架，通过三阶段流程（潜扩散生成 rolled 指纹、跨模态翻译为接触式指纹、基于物理的多姿态仿真）合成身份一致的接触式指纹多姿态样本，有效缩小接触式与非接触式指纹间的跨模态域差距，在UWA和PolyU CL2CB数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触式指纹识别虽具有卫生和采集灵活性的优势，但缺乏物理接触约束导致自由手指姿态引入严重非线性几何畸变，造成接触式与传统接触式指纹之间巨大的跨模态域差距。现有依赖显式几何校正或图像增强的方法在极端姿态变化下表现脆弱。

Method: IMPOSE包含三个阶段：(1) 利用潜扩散结合离散码本表示生成 rolled 指纹身份；(2) 通过受Sauvola局部自适应二值化引导的跨模态翻译，将rolled指纹转换为接触式模态；(3) 基于物理的3D手指模型纹理映射与投影进行多姿态仿真。

Result: 在UWA数据集上EER降至8.74%，在PolyU CL2CB数据集上EER降至2.26%；合成数据在DeepPrint、AFRNet等主流表示上均取得一致性能提升；混合使用合成与真实数据的策略达到最佳整体效果。

Conclusion: IMPOSE通过物理启发的多姿态合成方法有效解决了接触式指纹识别中的跨模态域差距问题，生成的样本在脊线拓扑层面保持严格身份一致性，并与标准指纹坐标空间对齐，为接触式指纹识别提供了一种有效的数据增强策略。

Abstract: Contactless fingerprint recognition has gained increasing attention due to its advantages in hygiene and acquisition flexibility. However, the absence of physical contact constraints introduces severe nonlinear geometric distortions caused by free finger poses in 3D space, resulting in a substantial cross-modal domain gap between contactless and conventional contact-based fingerprints. Existing solutions largely rely on explicit geometric correction or image enhancement, which are fragile under extreme pose variations. In this paper, we propose Identity-Consistent Multi-Pose Generation of Contactless Fingerprints (IMPOSE), a physics-inspired framework that synthesizes identity-preserving, multi-pose contactless fingerprint samples to empower recognition models. IMPOSE consists of three stages: (1) rolled fingerprint identity generation via latent diffusion with discrete codebook representations, (2) cross-modal translation from rolled to contactless modality guided by Sauvola-based local adaptive binarization as an identity anchor, and (3) physics-based multi-pose simulation through 3D finger model texture mapping and projection. The generated samples maintain strict identity consistency at the ridge topology level and spatial alignment with standard fingerprint coordinate space. Extensive experiments on the UWA and PolyU CL2CB databases demonstrate that fine-tuning fixed-length dense descriptors (FDD) with IMPOSE-synthesized data achieves state-of-the-art cross-modal matching, reducing EER to 8.74% on UWA and 2.26% on PolyU CL2CB. Synthetic data also yields consistent gains across mainstream representations including DeepPrint and AFRNet, and the hybrid strategy combining synthetic and real data achieves the best overall results. The code and generated samples are available at https://github.com/Yu-Yy/IMPOSE.

</details>


### [84] [Conditions for well-posed color recovery in scattering media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03837)
*Grigory Solomatov,Derya Akkaynak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了散射介质中场景颜色恢复的逆问题，指出该问题本质上是病态的（ill-posed），并提出了使颜色恢复成为良态（well-posed）的充分条件。作者证明，仅通过传感器改进无法解决介质引起的失真，而通过利用图像中自然存在的跨像素关系（恢复模式），在理想高光谱相机条件下可以约束解的唯一性。这为基于第一性原理的散射环境定量分析视觉算法开辟了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散射介质中的颜色恢复是一个基本逆问题，但由于多解性和预测误差不可控，该问题本质上是病态的。现有方法缺乏对候选解空间的系统理解，导致无法控制恢复精度。作者旨在寻找使该问题转变为良态的充分条件，从而为定量分析提供理论基础。

Method: 作者首先分析了病态性的两个根源：(i) 光谱信号到像素强度的投影，(ii) 未知的介质参数。然后证明仅改进传感器无法解决介质引起的失真。接着，作者识别出一种称为"恢复模式"的跨像素自然关系，并在理想高光谱相机条件下证明这些模式可以将解约束为唯一候选。

Result: 成功找到了使散射介质中颜色恢复成为良态问题的充分条件，即利用恢复模式作为额外约束，在理想高光谱相机设置下解是唯一的。

Conclusion: 该发现为基于第一性原理的视觉算法开辟了新方向，使得散射环境中的图像可以进行定量分析，突破了过去仅依赖传感器改进或经验方法的局限。

Abstract: Recovering scene color from images captured in scattering media is a fundamental inverse problem in optical imaging. Yet the problem is intrinsically ill-posed as multiple solutions can explain the same observation, and prediction error cannot be controlled without understanding the space of candidate solutions. Here, we present sufficient conditions under which color recovery in a scattering medium becomes well-posed. Observing that ill-posedness stems from (i) projection of spectral signals onto pixel intensities, and (ii) unknown medium parameters, we demonstrate that sensor improvements alone cannot resolve medium-induced distortions without additional constraints. We identify recovery patterns, cross-pixel relationships that naturally occur in images, and prove, for an ideal hyperspectral camera, that they restrict the solution to a unique candidate. This opens the door to a new class of vision algorithms grounded in first principles, enabling quantitative analysis of images in scattering environments.

</details>


### [85] [Parameter-Efficient Multi-View Proficiency Estimation: From Discriminative Classification to Generative Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03848)
*Edoardo Bianchi,Antonio Liotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文讨论了三种在多视角动作熟练度评估上的最新贡献：SkillFormer（参数高效判别式架构）、PATS（改进的时间采样策略）和ProfVLM（将熟练度评估转化为条件语言生成）。这些方法在Ego-Exo4D数据集上以更少的参数和训练轮次达到了SOTA精度，并推动了从封闭集分类向可解释反馈生成的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计一个人执行动作的熟练程度（而非动作类别）在教练指导、康复和人才识别中至关重要，但这一任务具有挑战性，因为熟练度编码在时序、平衡、身体力学和执行细节的微妙差异中，且通常分布在多个视角和短时事件中。

Method: SkillFormer采用参数高效判别架构进行选择性多视角融合；PATS通过保留局部密集的基础运动片段改进时间采样；ProfVLM通过门控跨视角投影器和小型语言主干，将熟练度评估重构为条件语言生成任务，同时输出熟练度标签和专家风格反馈。

Result: 在Ego-Exo4D上达到SOTA精度，可训练参数减少高达20倍，训练轮次减少高达3倍，相比视频Transformer基线。

Conclusion: 这些工作标志着向高效、多视角系统的转变，该系统结合了选择性融合、熟练度感知采样和可操作的生成式反馈，从封闭集分类迈向可解释的反馈生成。

Abstract: Estimating how well a person performs an action, rather than which action is performed, is central to coaching, rehabilitation, and talent identification. This task is challenging because proficiency is encoded in subtle differences in timing, balance, body mechanics, and execution, often distributed across multiple views and short temporal events. We discuss three recent contributions to multi-view proficiency estimation on Ego-Exo4D. SkillFormer introduces a parameter-efficient discriminative architecture for selective multi-view fusion; PATS improves temporal sampling by preserving locally dense excerpts of fundamental movements; and ProfVLM reformulates proficiency estimation as conditional language generation, producing both a proficiency label and expert-style feedback through a gated cross-view projector and a compact language backbone. Together, these methods achieve state-of-the-art accuracy on Ego-Exo4D with up to 20x fewer trainable parameters and up to 3x fewer training epochs than video-transformer baselines, while moving from closed-set classification toward interpretable feedback generation. These results highlight a shift toward efficient, multi-view systems that combine selective fusion, proficiency-aware sampling, and actionable generative feedback.

</details>


### [86] [Stream-R1: Reliability-Perplexity Aware Reward Distillation for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03849)
*Bin Wu,Mengqi Huang,Shaojin Wu,Weinan Jia,Yuxin Wang,Zhendong Mao,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stream-R1提出了一种可靠性-困惑度感知的奖励蒸馏框架，通过统一奖励引导机制在 rollout 和时空元素层面自适应重加权蒸馏目标，提升了流式视频扩散模型的蒸馏质量，在视觉质量、运动质量和文本对齐三个维度上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蒸馏方法对教师模型的输出不加区分地学习，忽略了DMD监督中两个互补的方差维度：不同学生rollout之间的可靠性差异，以及不同空间区域和时间帧中像素对质量改进贡献的不平等性。这种统一权重的目标函数混淆了"是否从每个rollout学习"和"在rollout内何处集中优化"两个问题。

Method: Stream-R1包含三个核心机制：(1) Inter-Reliability层：通过预训练视频奖励得分的指数缩放每个rollout的损失，使可靠监督占据主导；(2) Intra-Perplexity层：反向传播相同奖励模型提取逐像素梯度显著性，分解为空间和时间权重，将优化压力集中在可带来最大增益的区域和帧上；(3) 自适应平衡机制：防止视觉质量、运动质量和文本对齐中任何单一维度主导优化。

Result: 在标准流式视频生成基准上，Stream-R1在视觉质量、运动质量和文本对齐三个维度上均优于蒸馏基线方法，且无需修改架构或增加推理成本。

Conclusion: 通过可靠性-困惑度感知的奖励蒸馏框架，自适应重加权蒸馏目标，能够有效提升流式视频扩散模型的蒸馏质量，为实际部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Distillation-based acceleration has become foundational for making autoregressive streaming video diffusion models practical, with distribution matching distillation (DMD) as the de facto choice. Existing methods, however, train the student to match the teacher's output indiscriminately, treating every rollout, frame, and pixel as equally reliable supervision. We argue that this caps distilled quality, since it overlooks two complementary axes of variance in DMD supervision: Inter-Reliability across student rollouts whose supervision varies in reliability, and Intra-Perplexity across spatial regions and temporal frames that contribute unequally to where quality can still be improved. The objective thus conflates two questions under a uniform weight: whether to learn from each rollout, and where to concentrate optimization within it. To address this, we propose Stream-R1, a Reliability-Perplexity Aware Reward Distillation framework that adaptively reweights the distillation objective at both rollout and spatiotemporal-element levels through a single shared reward-guided mechanism. At the Inter-Reliability level, Stream-R1 rescales each rollout's loss by an exponential of a pretrained video reward score, so that rollouts with reliable supervision dominate optimization. At the Intra-Perplexity level, it back-propagates the same reward model to extract per-pixel gradient saliency, which is factored into spatial and temporal weights that concentrate optimization pressure on regions and frames where refinement yields the largest expected gain. An adaptive balancing mechanism prevents any single quality axis from dominating across visual quality, motion quality, and text alignment. Stream-R1 attains consistent improvements on all three dimensions over distillation baselines on standard streaming video generation benchmarks, without architectural modification or additional inference cost.

</details>


### [87] [DMGD: Train-Free Dataset Distillation with Semantic-Distribution Matching in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03877)
*Qichao Wang,Yunhong Lu,Hengyuan Cao,Junyi Zhang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需额外微调的双重匹配引导扩散框架（DMGD），通过语义匹配（条件似然优化）、动态引导机制和最优传输分布匹配，显著提升数据集蒸馏性能，在多个ImageNet子集上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的数据集蒸馏方法通常需要额外的微调阶段，且有效的引导机制尚未被充分探索，导致效率受限。

Method: 提出DMGD框架：1) 语义匹配：通过条件似然优化替代辅助分类器；2) 动态引导机制：增强合成数据多样性同时保持语义对齐；3) 基于最优传输的分布匹配：对齐目标分布结构；4) 两种高效策略：分布近似匹配和贪心渐进匹配。

Result: 在ImageNet-Woof、ImageNet-Nette和ImageNet-1K上，无需微调的方法分别比需要额外微调的SOTA方法平均准确率提升2.1%、5.4%和2.4%。

Conclusion: 提出的训练无需微调的扩散引导框架在数据集蒸馏任务上有效且高效，显著优于需要额外微调的现有方法。

Abstract: Dataset distillation enables efficient training by distilling the information of large-scale datasets into significantly smaller synthetic datasets. Diffusion based paradigms have emerged in recent years, offering novel perspectives for dataset distillation. However, they typically necessitate additional fine-tuning stages, and effective guidance mechanisms remain underexplored. To address these limitations, we rethink diffusion based dataset distillation and propose a Dual Matching Guided Diffusion (DMGD) framework, centered on efficient training-free guidance. We first establish Semantic Matching via conditional likelihood optimization, eliminating the need for auxiliary classifiers. Furthermore, we propose a dynamic guidance mechanism that enhances the diversity of synthetic data while maintaining semantic alignment. Simultaneously, we introduce an optimal transport (OT) based Distribution Matching approach to further align with the target distribution structure. To ensure efficiency, we develop two enhanced strategies for diffusion based framework: Distribution Approximate Matching and Greedy Progressive Matching. These strategies enable effective distribution matching guidance with minimal computational overhead. Experimental results on ImageNet-Woof, ImageNet-Nette, and ImageNet-1K demonstrate that our training-free approach achieves significant improvements, outperforming state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods requiring additional fine-tuning by average accuracy gains of 2.1%, 5.4%, and 2.4%, respectively.

</details>


### [88] [Raising the Ceiling: Better Empirical Fixation Densities for Saliency Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03885)
*Susmit Agrawal,Jannis Hollman,Matthias Kümmerer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自适应带宽KDE、中心偏差、均匀分量和先进显著性模型的混合方法，通过逐图像留一被试交叉验证优化参数，显著提高了注视密度估计的准确性，中位数每图像对数似然提升5-15%，AUC提升2个百分点，最受影响图像改进超25%，并揭示了现有显著性模型仍有较大改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注视密度估计（用于显著性基准测试）长期使用固定带宽各向同性高斯KDE方法，数十年未变。随着领域转向样本级评估（如失败案例分析、逆基准测试、逐图像模型比较），可靠的逐图像密度估计变得至关重要，亟需更精确的方法。

Method: 提出一种原理性混合模型，结合了基于Abramson方法的自适应带宽KDE、中心偏差分量、均匀分量以及一个先进的显著性模型，以捕获不同类型的空间和语义注视一致性，并通过留一被试交叉验证逐图像优化所有参数。

Result: 在多个基准测试上中位数每图像对数似然提升5-15%，AUC提升2个百分点；最受影响图像改进超25%；利用改进的估计识别并分析了当前最优显著性模型的剩余失败案例。

Conclusion: 经验注视密度不应被视为固定不变的基准真值，而是随方法改进而不断演化的估计；当前显著性模型仍有显著的改进空间（headroom）。

Abstract: Empirical fixation densities, spatial distributions estimated from human eye-tracking data, are foundational to saliency benchmarking. They directly shape benchmark conclusions, leaderboard rankings, failure case analyses, and scientific claims about human visual behavior. Yet the standard estimation method, fixed-bandwidth isotropic Gaussian KDE, has gone essentially unchanged for decades. This matters now more than ever: as the field shifts toward sample-level evaluation (failure case analysis, inverse benchmarking, per-image model comparison), reliable per-image density estimates become critical. We propose a principled mixture model that combines an adaptive-bandwidth KDE based on Abramson's method, center bias and uniform components, and a state-of-the-art saliency model, to capture different spatial and semantic types of interobserver consistency, and optimize all parameters per image via leave-one-subject-out cross-validation. Our method yields substantially higher interobserver consistency estimates across multiple benchmarks, with median per-image gains of 5-15% in log-likelihood and up to 2 percentage points in AUC. For the most affected images -- precisely those most relevant to failure case analysis -- improvements exceed 25%. We leverage these improved estimates to identify and analyze remaining failure cases of state-of-the-art saliency models, demonstrating that significant headroom for model improvement remains. More broadly, our findings highlight that empirical fixation densities should not be treated as fixed ground truths but as evolving estimates that improve with better methodology.

</details>


### [89] [StateVLM: A State-Aware Vision-Language Model for Robotic Affordance Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03927)
*Xiaowen Sun,Matthias Kerzel,Mengdi Li,Xufeng Zhao,Paul Striker,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Auxiliary Regression Loss (ARL)的训练策略，用于增强视觉语言模型在机器人任务中的数值推理能力（目标检测与状态定位），并开发了StateVLM模型以及OSAR基准数据集。实验表明ARL能显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在机器人应用中存在数值推理能力不足的问题，尤其是在目标检测和目标状态定位方面表现不佳，这限制了其在机器人任务中的实际应用效果。

Method: 提出ARL训练策略，在微调阶段利用框解码器输出计算辅助回归损失，推理时保持标准序列预测；基于此策略构建StateVLM模型，能够感知细粒度物体表示（精确定位、状态及可抓取区域）；同时构建了包含1,172个场景、7,746个物体的OSAR基准数据集。

Result: 在RefCOCO系列基准上，ARL使模型性能平均提升1.6%；在OSAR基准上，StateVLM+ARL相比无ARL版本平均提升5.2%。ARL在OSAR的复杂功能推理任务中能增强模型输出的一致性。

Conclusion: ARL训练策略有效提升了VLM在目标检测、状态定位等数值推理任务上的表现，StateVLM和OSAR数据集为机器人领域的物体状态功能推理提供了新的解决方案和评估基准。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown remarkable performance in various robotic tasks, as they can perceive visual information and understand natural language instructions. However, when applied to robotics, VLMs remain subject to a fundamental limitation inherent in large language models (LLMs): they struggle with numerical reasoning, particularly in object detection and object-state localization. To explore numerical reasoning as a regression task in VLMs, we propose a novel training strategy to adapt VLMs for object detection and object-state localization. This approach leverages box decoder outputs to compute an Auxiliary Regression Loss (ARL) during fine-tuning, while preserving standard sequence prediction at inference. We leverage this training strategy to develop StateVLM (State-aware Vision-Language Model), a novel model designed to perceive and learn fine-grained object representations, including precise localization of objects and their states, as well as graspable regions. Due to the lack of a benchmark for object-state affordance reasoning, we introduce an open-source benchmark, Object State Affordance Reasoning (OSAR), which contains 1,172 scenes with 7,746 individual objects and corresponding bounding boxes. Comparative experiments on adapted benchmarks (RefCOCO, RefCOCO+, and \mbox{RefCOCOg}) demonstrate that ARL improves model performance by an average of 1.6\% compared to models without ARL. Experiments on the OSAR benchmark further support this finding, showing that StateVLM with ARL achieves an average of 5.2\% higher performance than models without ARL. In particular, ARL is also important for the complex task of affordance reasoning in OSAR, where it enhances the consistency of model outputs.

</details>


### [90] [A Benchmark for Interactive World Models with a Unified Action Generation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03941)
*Jianjie Fang,Yingshan Lei,Qin Wan,Ziyou Wang,Yuchao Huang,Yongyan Xu,Baining Zhao,Weichen Zhang,Chen Gao,Xinlei Chen,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出iWorld-Bench，一个用于评估世界模型物理交互能力的大规模基准，包含33万视频片段、2.1k高质量样本和4.9k测试样本，覆盖6种任务类型，评估了14个世界模型，揭示了当前模型的关键局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究缺乏大规模数据集和统一基准来评估世界模型的物理交互能力，阻碍了AGI的发展。

Method: 构建33万视频片段数据集，筛选2.1k高质量样本；引入动作生成框架统一评估，设计6种任务类型（视觉生成、轨迹跟踪、记忆等），生成4.9k测试样本。

Result: 评估了14个代表性世界模型，识别出关键局限并提供未来研究见解。

Conclusion: iWorld-Bench为世界模型交互能力提供了统一评估基准，揭示了现有模型的不足，为AGI研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Achieving Artificial General Intelligence (AGI) requires agents that learn and interact adaptively, with interactive world models providing scalable environments for perception, reasoning, and action. Yet current research still lacks large-scale datasets and unified benchmarks to evaluate their physical interaction capabilities. To address this, we propose iWorld-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark for training and testing world models on interaction-related abilities such as distance perception and memory. We construct a diverse dataset with 330k video clips and select 2.1k high-quality samples covering varied perspectives, weather, and scenes. As existing world models differ in interaction modalities, we introduce an Action Generation Framework to unify evaluation and design six task types, generating 4.9k test samples. These tasks jointly assess model performance across visual generation, trajectory following, and memory. Evaluating 14 representative world models, we identify key limitations and provide insights for future research. The iWorld-Bench model leaderboard is publicly available at iWorld-Bench.com.

</details>


### [91] [Reservoir property image slices from the Groningen gas field for image translation and segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03942)
*Abdulrahman Al-Fakih,Nabil Sariah,Ardiansyah Koeshidayatullah,SanLinn I. Kaka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于Groningen静态地质模型的高分辨率储层属性图像数据集，包含相、孔隙度、渗透率和含水饱和度的对齐二维PNG图像，并提供了可复现的数据处理工作流，用于支持地质图像分析方法的基准测试和跨域关系研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储层表征工作流日益依赖基于图像和机器学习/深度学习方法，但公开可用的、适合可重复基准测试的地质图像数据集仍然有限。

Method: 从Groningen三维静态地质模型中提取储层属性切片，生成对齐的二维PNG图像（相、孔隙度、渗透率、含水饱和度），并构建可复现的软件工作流，包括数据增强、掩码生成、配对图像构建及基线实验。

Result: 生成了高分辨率对齐的储层属性图像数据集，并提供了完整的可复现处理工作流，支持可视化、分割和图像到图像翻译等下游任务。

Conclusion: 通过将固定图像数据集与可复现处理工作流分离，为地球科学、储层建模和机器学习应用提供了透明的基础设施，支持地质图像分析方法的基准测试和跨域属性关系研究。

Abstract: Reservoir characterization workflows increasingly rely on image-based and machine-learning/deep learning or even generative AI approaches, but openly available geological image datasets suitable for reproducible benchmarking remain limited. Here we describe a high-resolution dataset of reservoir-property image slices derived from the Groningen static geological model. The dataset contains aligned two-dimensional PNG images representing facies, porosity, permeability, and water saturation, generated from three-dimensional reservoir grids and prepared for downstream visualization, segmentation, and image-to-image translation tasks. In addition to the deposited original image corpus, we provide an archived software workflow for reproducing augmentation, mask generation, paired-image construction, and example baseline experiments. The resource is designed to support benchmarking of geological image analysis methods and the study of cross-domain relationships among reservoir properties. By separating the fixed image dataset from the reproducible processing workflow, this work provides a transparent foundation for reuse in geoscience, reservoir modeling, and machine-learning applications.

</details>


### [92] [UnAC: Adaptive Visual Prompting with Abstraction and Stepwise Checking for Complex Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03950)
*Yifan Wang,Yun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UnAC方法，一种多模态提示策略，通过自适应视觉提示、图像抽象提示和逐步自检机制，增强LMMs在复杂多模态任务中的多步推理能力，在MathVista、MM-Vet和MMMU基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMMs在视觉感知方面表现出色，但在需要多步推理的复杂视觉推理任务中仍不可靠，缺乏对视觉证据的系统性推理能力。

Method: 提出UnAC方法，包含三个关键组件：(1)自适应视觉提示策略，使LMMs聚焦于图像中的显著区域；(2)图像抽象提示，有效提取图像关键信息；(3)逐步自检机制，通过验证每个分解的子问题及其答案来改进推理过程。

Result: 在MathVista、MM-Vet和MMMU三个公开基准上进行了广泛实验，验证了UnAC方法的有效性。

Conclusion: UnAC作为一种多模态提示方法，能够有效增强GPT-4o、Gemini 1.5和GPT-4V等LMMs在复杂多模态任务中的推理能力，尤其提升了多步推理的可靠性。

Abstract: Although recent LMMs have become much stronger at visual perception, they remain unreliable on problems that require multi-step reasoning over visual evidence. In this paper, we present UnAC (Understanding, Abstracting, and Checking), a multimodal prompting method that strengthens reasoning for complex multimodal tasks in LMMs (e.g., GPT-4o, Gemini 1.5, and GPT-4V). To improve image understanding and capture fine details, we propose an adaptive visual prompting strategy that enables LMMs to focus on salient regions. We further design an image-abstraction prompt to effectively extract key information from images. In addition, we introduce a gradual self-checking scheme that improves reasoning by verifying each decomposed subquestion and its answer. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks-MathVista, MM-Vet, and MMMU.

</details>


### [93] [Enhanced 3D Brain Tumor Segmentation Using Assorted Precision Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04008)
*Adwaitt Pandya,Ozioma C. Oguine,Harita Bhargava,Shrikant Zade*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于SegResNet和自动混合精度训练的3D脑肿瘤分割方法，在BraTS数据集上取得了整体肿瘤0.90、肿瘤核心0.84、增强肿瘤0.79的Dice分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤是影响各年龄段人群的严重疾病，早期识别对患者生存至关重要。现有方法在三维分割的精度和效率上仍有提升空间，需要更先进的深度学习架构来实现准确可靠的肿瘤分割。

Method: 采用SegResNet架构进行三维分割，使用自动混合精度（AMP）训练策略，以Dice损失函数作为优化目标，Dice系数作为评价指标。

Result: 整体Dice分数为0.84；肿瘤核心Dice分数为0.84；整体肿瘤Dice分数为0.90；增强肿瘤Dice分数为0.79。

Conclusion: SegResNet结合自动混合精度训练能够有效实现脑肿瘤的三维分割，在整体肿瘤分割上表现优异，为脑肿瘤早期诊断提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: A brain tumor is a medical disorder faced by individuals of all demographics. Medically, it is described as the spread of non-essential cells close to or throughout the brain. Symptoms of this ailment include headaches, seizures, and sensory changes. This research explores two main categories of brain tumors: benign and malignant. Benign spreads steadily, and malignant expresses growth, making it dangerous. Early identification of brain tumors is a crucial factor for the survival of patients. This research provides a state-of-the-art approach to the early identification of tumors within the brain. We implemented the SegResNet architecture, a widely adopted architecture for three-dimensional segmentation, and trained it using the automatic multi-precision method. We incorporated the dice loss function and dice metric for evaluating the model. We got a dice score of 0.84. For the tumor core, we got a dice score of 0.84; for the whole tumor, 0.90; and for the enhanced tumor, we got a score of 0.79.

</details>


### [94] [Large-Scale High-Quality 3D Gaussian Head Reconstruction from Multi-View Captures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04035)
*Evangelos Ntavelis,Sean Wu,Mohamad Shahbazi,Fabio Maninchedda,Dmitry Kostiaev,Artem Sevastopolsky,Vittorio Megaro,Trevor Phillips,Alejandro Blumentals,Shridhar Ravikumar,Mehak Gupta,Reinhard Knothe,Jeronimo Bayer,Matthias Vestner,Simon Schaefer,Thomas Etterlin,Christian Zimmermann,Mathias Deschler,Peter Kaufmann,Stefan Brugger,Sebastian Martin,Brian Amberg,Tom Runia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HeadsUp是一种可扩展的前馈方法，用于从大规模多摄像头设置中重建高质量3D高斯人头。该方法采用编码器-解码器架构，将多视角输入压缩为紧凑潜在表示，再解码为UV参数化的3D高斯，附着在标准头部模板上。在超过10,000名受试者的内部数据集上训练，实现了最先进的重建质量，无需测试时优化即可泛化到新身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理多视角高分辨率输入时存在可扩展性问题，且缺乏大规模多视角人头数据集。Head-up的目标是开发一种能够高效处理大量高分辨率输入视图、可扩展到大规模数据集的3D人头重建方法。

Method: 采用编码器-解码器架构：编码器将多视角输入压缩为紧凑潜在表示；解码器将该表示解码为一组UV参数化的3D高斯，这些高斯附着在标准头部模板上。UV表示将3D高斯数量与输入图像的数量和分辨率解耦，使得可以使用大量高分辨率输入视图进行训练。

Result: 在超过10,000名受试者的内部数据集上（比现有多视角人头数据集大一个数量级）训练和评估，实现了最先进的重建质量，能够泛化到新身份而无需测试时优化。此外，还深入分析了模型在身份、视图和模型容量方面的扩展行为，揭示了质量与计算之间的实用权衡。

Conclusion: HeadsUp通过UV参数化的3D高斯表示和编码器-解码器架构，成功实现了可扩展的高质量3D人头重建。其潜在空间还支持两个下游应用：生成新的3D身份和通过表情混合形状驱动3D人头动画。

Abstract: We propose HeadsUp, a scalable feed-forward method for reconstructing high-quality 3D Gaussian heads from large-scale multi-camera setups. Our method employs an efficient encoder-decoder architecture that compresses input views into a compact latent representation. This latent representation is then decoded into a set of UV-parameterized 3D Gaussians anchored to a neutral head template. This UV representation decouples the number of 3D Gaussians from the number and resolution of input images, enabling training with many high-resolution input views. We train and evaluate our model on an internal dataset with more than 10,000 subjects, which is an order of magnitude larger than existing multi-view human head datasets. HeadsUp achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction quality and generalizes to novel identities without test-time optimization. We extensively analyze the scaling behavior of our model across identities, views, and model capacity, revealing practical insights for quality-compute trade-offs. Finally, we highlight the strength of our latent space by showcasing two downstream applications: generating novel 3D identities and animating the 3D heads with expression blendshapes.

</details>


### [95] [Audio-Visual Intelligence in Large Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04045)
*You Qin,Kai Liu,Shengqiong Wu,Kai Wang,Shijian Deng,Yapeng Tian,Junbin Xiao,Yazhou Xing,Yinghao Ma,Bobo Li,Roger Zimmermann,Lei Cui,Furu Wei,Jiebo Luo,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对基于大基础模型的音视频智能（AVI）领域进行了首次全面综述，构建了涵盖理解、生成、交互三类任务的统一分类体系，系统梳理了模态分词、跨模态融合、自回归/扩散生成、大规模预训练、指令对齐和偏好优化等方法论基础，并整理了代表性数据集、基准和评估指标，识别了同步性、空间推理、可控性和安全性等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频智能（AVI）已成为人工智能的前沿方向，尤其是在大基础模型时代，音频与视觉的联合建模对于理解、可控生成和跨时间信号推理至关重要。然而，现有文献碎片化严重，涉及多种任务、不一致的分类法和异构评估实践，阻碍了系统比较和知识整合，亟需一份统一的综述来梳理该领域。

Method: 本文采用综述研究方法，建立了统一的AVI任务分类体系（理解、生成、交互三大类），系统整合了方法论基础（模态分词、跨模态融合、自回归与扩散生成、大规模预训练、指令对齐、偏好优化），并整理了代表性数据集、基准和评估指标，进行跨任务族的结构化比较。

Result: 形成了首个面向大基础模型的AVI全面综述，提供了统一的任务分类框架、方法论综合、数据集/基准/评估指标整理，以及开放挑战（同步性、空间推理、可控性、安全性）的系统识别。

Conclusion: 通过将快速发展的AVI领域整合为连贯框架，本文旨在为未来大规模AVI研究提供基础性参考，推动该领域向更系统化的方向发展。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Intelligence (AVI) has emerged as a central frontier in artificial intelligence, bridging auditory and visual modalities to enable machines that can perceive, generate, and interact in the multimodal real world. In the era of large foundation models, joint modeling of audio and vision has become increasingly crucial, i.e., not only for understanding but also for controllable generation and reasoning across dynamic, temporally grounded signals. Recent advances, such as Meta MovieGen and Google Veo-3, highlight the growing industrial and academic focus on unified audio-vision architectures that learn from massive multimodal data. However, despite rapid progress, the literature remains fragmented, spanning diverse tasks, inconsistent taxonomies, and heterogeneous evaluation practices that impede systematic comparison and knowledge integration. This survey provides the first comprehensive review of AVI through the lens of large foundation models. We establish a unified taxonomy covering the broad landscape of AVI tasks, ranging from understanding (e.g., speech recognition, sound localization) to generation (e.g., audio-driven video synthesis, video-to-audio) and interaction (e.g., dialogue, embodied, or agentic interfaces). We synthesize methodological foundations, including modality tokenization, cross-modal fusion, autoregressive and diffusion-based generation, large-scale pretraining, instruction alignment, and preference optimization. Furthermore, we curate representative datasets, benchmarks, and evaluation metrics, offering a structured comparison across task families and identifying open challenges in synchronization, spatial reasoning, controllability, and safety. By consolidating this rapidly expanding field into a coherent framework, this survey aims to serve as a foundational reference for future research on large-scale AVI.

</details>

### [96] [Safety in Embodied AI: A Survey of Risks, Attacks, and Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02900)
*Xiao Li,Xiang Zheng,Yifeng Gao,Xinyu Xia,Yixu Wang,Xin Wang,Ye Sun,Yunhan Zhao,Ming Wen,Jiayu Li,Xun Gong,Yi Liu,Yige Li,Yutao Wu,Cong Wang,Jun Sun,Yixin Cao,Zhineng Chen,Jingjing Chen,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Zuxuan Wu,Xipeng Qiu,Xuanjing Huang,Tiehua Zhang,Zhipeng Wei,Hanxun Huang,Sarah Erfani,James Bailey,Jianping Wang,Wei-Ying Ma,Bo Li,Xingjun Ma,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了具身人工智能（Embodied AI）在感知、认知、规划、行动与交互全流程中的安全研究，涵盖攻击与防御两大方面，提出了多层次分类体系，并识别了多模态感知融合脆弱性、规划层越狱攻击不稳定性、人机交互可信度等关键未被充分研究的挑战，为构建安全可靠的具身智能体提供了路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI系统在开放世界、安全关键环境（如交通、医疗、工业机器人）中运行时，其故障可能导致物理伤害，现有的数字AI安全研究无法直接适用于具身场景，亟需对具身AI安全问题进行全面系统的梳理。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，从400余篇论文中提取并分类，提出了覆盖具身AI全流程（感知、认知、规划、行动与交互、智能体系统）的多层次安全分类学，统一了对抗攻击、后门攻击、越狱攻击、硬件攻击、攻击检测、安全训练、鲁棒推理以及风险感知人机交互等分散的研究线索。

Result: 总结了具身AI各环节的安全攻防现状，揭示了多模态感知融合的脆弱性、规划层在越狱攻击下的不稳定性、以及开放场景下人机交互的可信度等被忽视的挑战。

Conclusion: 通过构建统一框架和识别研究空白，该综述为构建既能自主又能安全、鲁棒、可靠地部署于真实世界的具身智能体提供了路线图。

Abstract: Embodied Artificial Intelligence (Embodied AI) integrates perception, cognition, planning, and interaction into agents that operate in open-world, safety-critical environments. As these systems gain autonomy and enter domains such as transportation, healthcare, and industrial or assistive robotics, ensuring their safety becomes both technically challenging and socially indispensable. Unlike digital AI systems, embodied agents must act under uncertain sensing, incomplete knowledge, and dynamic human-robot interactions, where failures can directly lead to physical harm. This survey provides a comprehensive and structured review of safety research in embodied AI, examining attacks and defenses across the full embodied pipeline, from perception and cognition to planning, action and interaction, and agentic system. We introduce a multi-level taxonomy that unifies fragmented lines of work and connects embodied-specific safety findings with broader advances in vision, language, and multimodal foundation models. Our review synthesizes insights from over 400 papers spanning adversarial, backdoor, jailbreak, and hardware-level attacks; attack detection, safe training and robust inference; and risk-aware human-agent interaction. This analysis reveals several overlooked challenges, including the fragility of multimodal perception fusion, the instability of planning under jailbreak attacks, and the trustworthiness of human-agent interaction in open-ended scenarios. By organizing the field into a coherent framework and identifying critical research gaps, this survey provides a roadmap for building embodied agents that are not only capable and autonomous but also safe, robust, and reliable in real-world deployment.

</details>


### [97] [Tracing the Dynamics of Refusal: Exploiting Latent Refusal Trajectories for Robust Jailbreak Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02958)
*Xulin Hu,Che Wang,Wei Yang Bryan Lim,Jianbo Gao,Zhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过因果追踪发现拒绝机制是一个动态稀疏过程而非静态终端结果，并提出了SALO（稀疏激活定位算子）作为推理时检测器，利用上游的"拒绝轨迹"信号，在终端信号被对抗攻击压制时仍能恢复防御能力，将强制解码攻击检测率从约0%提升至>90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表征工程方法依赖终端表示的静态拒绝向量，但这种方式容易被对抗攻击（如GCG）压制。作者希望突破这一范式，探索拒绝机制的本质动态过程，并设计更鲁棒的防御方法。

Method: 使用因果追踪（Causal Tracing）揭示模型内部的"拒绝轨迹"（Refusal Trajectory），即存在于上游层的持久性拒绝信号。基于此发现，提出SALO（Sparse Activation Localization Operator），一种推理时检测器，用于捕获这些潜在的稀疏激活模式。

Result: SALO在强制解码攻击场景下，将检测率从依赖终端状态方法的约0%提升至超过90%，有效恢复了防御能力。

Conclusion: 拒绝机制本质上是一个动态的稀疏过程，而非局部化的终端输出。利用上游的拒绝轨迹信号可以构建更鲁棒的安全防御，尤其是在终端信号被对抗攻击完全压制的情况下。

Abstract: Representation Engineering typically relies on static refusal vectors derived from terminal representations. We move beyond this paradigm, demonstrating that refusal is a dynamic and sparse process rather than a localized outcome. Using Causal Tracing, we uncover the Refusal Trajectory-a persistent upstream signature that remains intact even when adversarial attacks (e.g., GCG) suppress terminal signals. Leveraging this, we propose SALO (Sparse Activation Localization Operator), an inference-time detector designed to capture these latent patterns. SALO effectively recovers defense capabilities against forced-decoding attacks, improving detection rates from ~0% to >90% where methods relying on terminal states perform poorly.

</details>


### [98] [Contrastive Privacy: A Semantic Approach to Measuring Privacy of AI-based Sanitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02977)
*George Bissias,Eugene Bagdasarian,Brian Neil Levine*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出对比隐私（Contrastive Privacy）——一种形式化的隐私定义，用于对经过语义概念清洗的多模态媒体（图像与文本）进行系统性、定量化的隐私保护评估，无需人工标注，并可在前沿模型上量化清洗效果与识别具体失败案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于AI的语义级隐私清洗方法（如移除人脸、衣物、体型等）在实践中日益流行，但其评估方式多为定制化、缺乏形式化保证。亟需一种独立于模型与机制的、可跨模态的统一隐私测试框架。

Method: 提出对比隐私（Contrastive Privacy）的形式化定义，利用CLIP等模型作为语义度量，通过对比清洗后的媒体与同语料库中其他图像的潜在语义差异来判断隐私是否被有效移除，无需人工标注。

Result: 在图像上测试34种模型组合，在文本上测试15种模型，量化了不同组合下的清洗成功率，并能识别出清洗语料中的具体失败案例。

Conclusion: 对比隐私提供了一种独立于具体清洗机制（如像素暗化、模糊或更高级混淆方法）的通用评估框架，在理想条件下具有形式化保证，且可通过不完美的语义度量在实际中有效运作。

Abstract: To sanitize specific concepts from imagery and text, privacy mechanisms with formal guarantees are often eschewed in practice in favor of more intuitive techniques. AI-based sanitization is poised to grow in popularity because it can work with the semantics of natural language concepts; e.g., a prompt to "remove faces, clothing, and body shape". Many approaches exist commercially and as prior work. But, the evaluation of such approaches has been bespoke and without formal guarantees.
  To fill this gap, we propose contrastive privacy, a formal definition of privacy that provides a systematic and quantitative test of sanitized media that has a semantic interpretation. It is independent of the model and mechanism used and operates across multiple media modalities. Contrastive privacy provides guarantees under ideal conditions; and we show how to operationalize the definition with imperfect measures of semantics, provided by models like CLIP, that can connect concepts latently. Notably, the algorithm contrasts sanitized media with other images from the same corpus to arrive at a determination; no manual labeling is involved.
  In our experiments, we apply our privacy test to both images and text using frontier models: some generate concepts to sanitize and others perform the sanitization. With our test we quantify sanitization success across 34 combinations of models on images, and for 15 models on text. The approach not only quantifies success overall, it identifies specific failures from a sanitized corpus. Further, it is independent of the mechanism used for sanitization, whether by darkening pixels, blurring, or applying more advanced means of obfuscation.

</details>


### [99] [Observability for Post-Quantum TLS Readiness: A Multi-Surface Evidence Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02978)
*José Luis Delgado*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一个面向后量子TLS可观测性的多层面框架，该框架区分被动会话证据、主动探测、证书链证据和注册表知识，并在29个受控场景和1000个目标的大规模评估中验证了其有效性，能发现单一经典会话视图无法揭示的端点能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLS后量子迁移需要证据感知的测量方法，以区分会话协商、端点能力、证书链证据和缺失观测的来源，这在TLS 1.3加密、恢复、双向TLS、截断、分片、合并、主动证书检索和时间漂移等复杂场景下至关重要。

Method: 提出了一个多层面框架，将被动会话证据、主动探测、证书链证据和注册表知识分离，并映射到会话行为、密钥建立、端点能力、认证、生命周期、可观测性和策略等多个测量平面。框架通过模式强制观察和结果、版本化注册表、可审计推理规则、压力契约和基线适配器实现为可复现工件。

Result: 在29个受控场景中，被动证据可闭合会话级平面，主动探测建立能力下界，多层面证据闭合完整测量对象。基线量子漏洞分析器仅检测到29次运行中的2次和23次TLS 1.3运行中的0次。在大规模评估中完成1971次握手，收集1368个链工件，确认310个目标支持混合能力，发现310个端点能力超越单一经典会话视图的情况。

Conclusion: 多层面证据融合方法能够有效揭示传统单视图无法观测的端点后量子能力，对未来大规模后量子TLS迁移部署中的可观测性具有重要价值。

Abstract: Post-quantum migration in Transport Layer Security (TLS) requires evidence-aware measurements that distinguish session negotiation, endpoint capability, certificate-chain evidence, and the provenance of missing observations. This distinction is essential under TLS 1.3 encryption, resumption, mutual TLS, trace truncation, fragmentation, coalescing, active certificate retrieval, and temporal drift.
  We present a multi-surface framework for post-quantum TLS observability. The framework separates passive session evidence, active probing, certificate-chain evidence, and registry knowledge, and maps them onto measurement planes for session behavior, key establishment, endpoint capability, authentication, lifecycle, observability, and policy. We instantiate it as a reproducible artifact with schema-enforced observations and results, versioned registries, auditable inference rules, stress contracts, and baseline adapters.
  We evaluate the framework on 29 controlled scenarios spanning TLS 1.2 and TLS 1.3, classical and hybrid key establishment, mutual TLS, resumption, HelloRetryRequest, truncation, fragmentation and coalescing, temporal drift, IPv6, and chain-depth variation. Passive evidence closes session-level planes, active probing establishes capability lower bounds, and multi-surface evidence closes the full measurement object while preserving uncertainty and contradiction when required. Against an inherited TLS quantum-vulnerability analyzer, the baseline detects 2 of 29 runs and 0 of 23 TLS 1.3 runs. In a stratified public campaign over 1000 targets and 2000 fresh probes, the framework completes 1971 handshakes, collects 1368 chain artifacts, confirms hybrid capability for 310 targets, and identifies 310 cases where endpoint capability exceeds what any single classical session view reveals.

</details>


### [100] [Towards a Risk-Cost Model for Financial Adaptive Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02979)
*Supriya Khadka,Sanchari Das*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向金融系统自适应认证的形式化风险-成本模型（RCM），该模型将欺诈损失、机会成本、尾部风险（CVaR）、序贯决策机制以及隐私与监管约束统一纳入动态优化框架，使认证系统从静态分类与合规驱动转向经济基础扎实、尾部风险敏感且对抗不确定性下具有鲁棒性的设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融系统自适应认证在概念上碎片化，往往优先考虑监管合规而非显式的经济和对抗风险建模，这种结构性失衡导致即使微小的误接受率增加也可能造成灾难性资金损失。

Method: 提出形式化的风险-成本模型（RCM），包含三个核心组件：(i) 基于条件风险价值（CVaR）的成本敏感风险函数，刻画欺诈损失、机会成本和尾部风险；(ii) 适应对抗试探和分布漂移的序贯决策机制；(iii) 将隐私和监管约束嵌入优化目标。

Result: 将认证问题重新表述为一个受约束的动态风险-成本优化问题，实现了经济上有依据、尾部风险敏感且在对抗不确定性下具有鲁棒性的认证系统。

Conclusion: RCM超越了静态分类和合规驱动的设计范式，为金融系统自适应认证提供了数学上严谨、经济上合理且具有对抗鲁棒性的理论基础。

Abstract: Authentication in financial systems remains a uniquely high-stakes security challenge, where even marginal increases in false acceptance can result in catastrophic monetary loss. Existing deployments of adaptive authentication, which combine biometrics, behavioral signals, and contextual risk scoring, remain conceptually fragmented and often prioritize regulatory compliance over explicit economic and adversarial risk modeling. To address this structural imbalance, in this paper we introduce a formal Risk-Cost Model (RCM) for adaptive authentication in financial systems. The RCM provides a principled mathematical foundation that integrates three essential components: (i) cost-sensitive risk functions that explicitly capture fraud loss, opportunity cost, and tail risk through Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR); (ii) sequential decision-making mechanisms that adapt to adversarial probing and distributional drift; and (iii) quantifiable privacy and regulatory constraints embedded directly within the optimization objective. By reframing authentication as a constrained dynamic risk-cost optimization problem, the RCM moves beyond static classification and compliance-driven design toward systems that are economically grounded, tail-risk aware, and resilient under adversarial uncertainty.

</details>


### [101] [LiteShield: Hybrid Feature Selection-Driven Lightweight Intrusion Detection for Resource-Constrained IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02987)
*Dileepa Mabulage,Banuka Athuraliya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LiteShield，一种结合混合特征选择（MI+RFECV）与轻量级分类器的物联网入侵检测系统。实验表明，KNN在二分类（98.26%）和多分类（85.22%）上准确率最高，但随机森林在检测质量与部署效率间取得最佳权衡（二分类98.01%，多分类80.39%），模型规模与推理成本显著更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备的快速扩张导致攻击面增大，而现有入侵检测系统大多计算开销过高，无法适配资源受限的物联网环境，存在安全匹配缺口。

Method: 基于UNSW-NB15数据集，进行数据预处理与不平衡感知准备，采用两阶段特征选择流程（互信息MI + 递归特征消除与交叉验证RFECV），评估六种轻量级分类器（决策树、随机森林、KNN、逻辑回归、朴素贝叶斯、支持向量机）在二分类和多分类场景下的性能。

Result: KNN在原始预测性能上最佳（二分类98.26%，多分类85.22%）；随机森林在检测质量与部署效率间取得最优权衡（二分类98.01%，多分类80.39%），模型规模和推理成本远低于KNN。消融分析表明类别不平衡显著影响多分类性能。

Conclusion: 混合特征选择与轻量级机器学习可为资源受限的物联网环境提供准确且计算可行的入侵检测方案。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of Internet of Things (IoT) deployments has enlarged the attack surface of modern digital infrastructure while exposing a key security mismatch: many intrusion detection systems (IDSs) remain too computationally expensive for constrained IoT environments. This paper presents LiteShield, a lightweight machine learning-based IDS that combines hybrid feature selection with efficient classifiers to support accurate attack detection under limited computational budgets. The proposed framework uses the UNSW-NB15 dataset, applies data preprocessing and imbalance-aware preparation, and employs a two-stage feature selection pipeline based on Mutual Information (MI) and Recursive Feature Elimination with Cross-Validation (RFECV). Six lightweight classifiers are evaluated for both binary and multiclass intrusion detection: Decision Tree, Random Forest, K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN), Logistic Regression, Naïve Bayes, and Support Vector Machine.
  Experimental results show that KNN achieved the highest raw predictive performance, reaching 98.26% accuracy for binary classification and 85.22% accuracy for multiclass classification. However, Random Forest delivered the most practical trade-off between detection quality and deployment efficiency, obtaining 98.01% binary accuracy and 80.39% multiclass accuracy with substantially lower model size and inference cost than KNN. Additional ablation analysis on minority attack classes indicates that class imbalance materially affects multiclass performance. Overall, LiteShield demonstrates that hybrid feature selection and lightweight machine learning can provide a viable path toward accurate and computationally feasible intrusion detection for IoT-focused environments.

</details>


### [102] [ChaRVoC: A Challenge-Response Voice Cancelable Authentication System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02990)
*Phuc-Khang Vo-Hoang,Hoang C. Ta,Nhien-An Le-Khac,Dinh-Thuc Nguyen,Hong-Hanh Nguyen-Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ChaRVoC系统，一种基于挑战-响应的语音可撤销认证系统，通过HashGray-XOR方案结合语音生物特征、用户密钥和系统挑战，实现防重放攻击、模板可撤销性和不可逆性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音认证系统面临重放攻击、模板不可撤销和模板泄露等问题，亟需一种集防重放、可撤销和不可逆性于一体的安全方案。

Method: 提出HashGray-XOR方案，将密码学哈希函数与基于格雷码的不可逆变换结合，生成数学上不可逆的安全模板；整合三重安全因子：语音生物特征、用户记忆密钥（实现模板可撤销性）、系统动态挑战（实现活体检测）。

Result: 在VoxCeleb1、TIMIT和VOiCES数据集上，与WTA、IoM、RoE等现有可撤销生物特征方法对比，展示了系统识别性能；系统同时实现了可撤销性和不可链接性。

Conclusion: ChaRVoC系统有效解决了语音认证中的重放攻击、模板可撤销性和模板泄露问题，具备可撤销性和不可链接性，且识别性能具有竞争力。

Abstract: In this work, we present a Challenge-Response Voice Cancelable authentication system, called ChaRVoC, which provides protection against replay attacks, revocability issues, and template compromise. Our approach integrates three security factors: (1) inherent voice biometric characteristics, (2) user-memorized secret keys enabling template revocability, and (3) dynamic system-generated challenges providing liveness detection. Specifically, we introduce a novel HashGray-XOR scheme which combines a cryptographic hash function with an unrecoverable graycode-based transformation to create secured templates that are mathematically proven to be non-invertible. We compare our methods with existing cancelable biometric methods (WTA, IoM, RoE) on VoxCeleb1, TIMIT, and VOiCES datasets to show the recognition performance of our proposed system. We also show that our system achieves both cancelability and unlinkability properties.

</details>


### [103] [PHANTOM: Polymorphic Honeytoken Adaptation with Narrative-Tailored Organisational Mimicry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02992)
*Abraham Itzhak Weinberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了PHANTOM框架，通过编码组织特定知识生成上下文逼真的蜜令牌，在可信度评分上显著优于传统模板方法（B=0.778 vs 0.576），人类接受率从6.2%提升至100%，检测抵抗率从0.609提升至0.870，且无需外部API调用即可在隔离环境中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蜜令牌生成工具产生静态、基于模板的令牌，缺乏组织特异性，易被统计、句法和语义分析识别。需要一种能生成具有组织上下文逼真度的动态蜜令牌方法。

Method: PHANTOM框架将组织特定知识（域名、服务命名惯例、技术栈习惯用语、真实秘密值分布）编码到多组件生成流水线中；形式化定义了一个四组件可信度评分（句法有效性、语义连贯性、统计合理性、人类接受度）来评估蜜令牌质量。

Result: 在8种令牌类型和4种组织上下文中，PHANTOM的B=0.778±0.057 vs 模板基线B=0.576±0.058（Δ=+0.203, t=14.07, p<0.001, Cohen's d=3.52）；人类评估者接受率从6.2%提升至100%；检测抵抗率从0.609提升至0.870（三种模拟扫描器均有p<0.001）；语义连贯性差距（ΔSc=+0.309, d=4.52）是主要驱动力。

Conclusion: 组织上下文是当前蜜令牌方法缺失的关键要素；PHANTOM无需外部API调用即可完全在气隙环境中部署和复现。

Abstract: Honeytokens, decoy digital assets planted to detect and attribute unauthorised access, are a well-established primitive in cyber deception. Existing generation tools produce static, template-based tokens that lack organisational specificity and are identifiable by statistical, syntactic, and semantic analysis. We introduce PHANTOM (Polymorphic Honeytoken Adaptation with Narrative-Tailored Organisational Mimicry), a framework that generates contextually convincing honeytokens by encoding organisation-specific knowledge: domain names, service naming conventions, technology-stack idioms, and realistic secret-value distributions, into a multi-component generation pipeline. We formalise honeytoken quality through a four-component Believability Score that captures syntactic validity, semantic coherence, statistical plausibility, and human acceptance. We use this metric to evaluate PHANTOM across 8 token types and 4 organisational contexts against a template-based baseline. PHANTOM achieves B = 0.778 +/- 0.057 versus B = 0.576 +/- 0.058 for templates (Delta = +0.203, t = 14.07, p < 0.001, Cohen's d = 3.52). Human-evaluator acceptance rises from 6.2% to 100%, and detection resistance (DR = 1 - Pd) improves from 0.609 to 0.870 across three simulated scanner models (regex, entropy analysis, and ML classifier), each with p < 0.001. The semantic coherence gap (Delta Sc = +0.309, d = 4.52) is the dominant driver, confirming the hypothesis that organisational context is the critical missing ingredient in current approaches. All results are reproduced without external API calls, making the pipeline fully deployable in air-gapped environments.

</details>


### [104] [Revisiting JBShield: Breaking and Rebuilding Representation-Level Jailbreak Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03095)
*Kemal Derya,Berk Sunar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出JB-GCG攻击，针对JBShield防御在自适应威胁模型下实现高达53.4%的攻击成功率，并揭示其结构性漏洞。随后提出基于多层表示一致性（马氏距离异常检测）的新防御RTV，将自适应攻击成功率压制至7%。研究表明单层概念相似性检测在自适应攻击下不可靠，多层表示一致性才是更鲁棒的检测基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御如JBShield在非自适应设置下宣称0%攻击成功率，但其鲁棒性在自适应威胁模型（攻击者知晓并针对性攻击防御机制）下未经充分验证。需要检验这类防御在更现实的威胁场景中的有效性。

Method: 1) JB-GCG攻击：修改GCG目标函数，结合拒绝方向抑制（余弦相似度）与JBShield自身的有毒概念正则化。2) RTV防御：基于多层拒绝方向指纹的马氏距离异常检测。

Result: JB-GCG在Llama-3-8B上平均ASR 46.2%，最高53.4%；对JBShield-M达30.7%。RTV防御AUROC达0.99；自适应攻击在13倍计算成本下仅7% ASR。

Conclusion: 强非自适应检测不意味着自适应威胁下的鲁棒性；多层表示一致性比单层概念相似性更可靠；RTV可作为更具鲁棒性的防御基线的基线。

Abstract: Defending large language models (LLMs) against jailbreak attacks, such as Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG), remains a challenge, particularly under adaptive threat models where an attacker directly targets the defense mechanism. JBShield, a recent jailbreak defense with a 0% attack success rate in some settings, detects malicious prompts via two concept signals, a toxic concept and a jailbreak concept. We design JB-GCG, which modifies GCG's objective to combine two terms: refusal-direction suppression via cosine similarity between the refusal direction and hidden-state representations, and toxic-concept regularization via JBShield's own toxic concept score. Across five configurations on Llama-3-8B, JB-GCG achieves an average ASR of 46.2%, reaching up to 53.4% in the strongest setting. We further show that our attack remains effective against JBShield-M, achieving ASR up to 30.7% across evaluated settings. The attack persists across multiple JBShield recalibrations, confirming that the vulnerability is structural rather than calibration-specific. We analyze the cosine-similarity signatures of jailbreak representations and find that they occupy a distinctive region in refusal-direction fingerprint space that neither harmless nor harmful prompts inhabit. We introduce Representation Trajectory Verification (RTV), a new defense based on Mahalanobis outlier detection over multi-layer refusal-direction fingerprints. RTV attains an AUROC of 0.99 against our attack. Finally, we design and evaluate an additional adaptive attack against RTV with full white-box knowledge of the defense; the best attack achieves only 7% ASR at 13x the computational cost. Our results show that strong non-adaptive detection does not imply robustness under adaptive threat models, and that multi-layer representation consistency is a more reliable foundation for jailbreak detection than single-layer concept similarity.

</details>


### [105] [Zero Day Attacks: Novel Behaviour or Novel Vulnerability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03138)
*Nnamdi Jibunoh,Sara Khanchi,Adetokunbo Makanju*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对20年真实零日攻击事件的回顾，发现零日攻击源于未公开漏洞的利用而非新颖攻击行为。现有ML入侵检测系统过度关注"新颖行为"检测，与真实攻击机制存在错配。作者主张以漏洞为中心的方法，并呼吁改进自动化漏洞检测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零日攻击具有高成功率和隐蔽性，传统签名方法难以检测。现有ML-based IDS声称能检测零日攻击，但其设计假设与真实攻击机制存在偏差，需要重新审视检测范式的有效性。

Method: 回顾20年间有记录的零日攻击事件，分析漏洞利用模式；提出零日漏洞类型分类法；评估ML入侵检测方法的假设与真实攻击数据的一致性。

Result: 1) 零日攻击始终涉及漏洞利用，内存破坏漏洞最常用；2) 针对防御机制漏洞的攻击近年增多；3) 许多ML检测器针对"新颖行为"设计，但真实攻击中行为并不新颖；4) 漏洞中心方法更贴合真实攻击机制。

Conclusion: 行为检测不能等同于零日攻击检测；应优先发展漏洞中心方法，实现攻击生命周期的早期识别与缓解；需谨慎解读ML-based IDS的零日检测能力宣称。

Abstract: Zero-day attacks pose severe cybersecurity risks due to their high success rates and stealth. Because signature-based approaches struggle to detect such attacks, building Intrusion Detection Systems (IDSs) for detecting zero-day attacks is essential. We contend that for an IDS to be effective it must be grounded in an understanding of how zero-day attacks manifest in real-world networks. To this end, we review documented zero-day incidents spanning 20 years, finding that these attacks arise from the exploitation of undisclosed vulnerabilities rather than novel attack behavior. Guided by this insight, we propose a taxonomy of zero-day vulnerability types and analyze assumptions of ML-based intrusion detection approaches. Our analysis shows that incidents consistently involve vulnerability exploitation, with memory-corruption flaws being most used; additionally, attacks targeting defensive-mechanism vulnerabilities have increased in recent years. We also identify a mismatch: while incident reports emphasize vulnerability exploitation, many ML-based detectors are designed to detect hypothetical "novel behaviors" during attack execution. Our findings indicate that vulnerability-centric methods are more aligned with real-world attack mechanisms. Consequently, reliance on behavior-based detection alone may overstate zero-day detection capabilities in ML-based IDSs. We advocate for cautious interpretation of such claims and call for improved automated vulnerability detection frameworks aligned with real-world exploit characteristics. Effective defense against zero-day attacks requires prioritizing vulnerability-centeric approaches that enable early identification and mitigation across the lifecycle. The ability to detect attacks that utilize novel behaviors (Tactics, Techniques, and Procedures (TTPs)) is useful, but it does necessarily equate to the ability to detect zero-day attacks.

</details>


### [106] [Evaluating Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Explainable Malware Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03140)
*Jayson Ng,Amin Milani Fard*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文实证评估了RAG在恶意软件解释中的效果，发现当已有结构化安全证据时，RAG反而会降低解释质量，引入干扰信息和叙述噪声。作者认为恶意软件解释本质上是信号提取任务而非知识检索问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证"RAG能通过注入外部安全知识提升解释质量"这一常见假设，因为LLM正越来越多地被用作安全工程工具来总结和解释恶意软件行为。

Method: 以VirusTotal报告作为结构化输入，在多种LLM上实证评估RAG对恶意软件解释质量的影响。

Result: RAG频繁降低解释质量，表现为引入分散注意力或弱相关的上下文，以及增加叙述噪声或泛泛的总结内容。

Conclusion: 恶意软件解释主要是信号提取任务而非知识检索问题，在已有足够结构化安全证据的情况下，RAG可能适得其反。论文提出了安全开发工作流的设计建议。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being used as security engineering tools to summarize and explain malware behavior to analysts. A common assumption is that Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) improves explanation quality by injecting external security knowledge. In this work, we empirically evaluate this assumption for malware explanation using VirusTotal reports as structured input. Across multiple LLMs, we find that RAG frequently degrades explanation quality by introducing distracting or weakly related context and adding narrative noise or generic write-ups. Our results highlight a practical risk in security-critical pipelines for malware explanation that RAG can be counterproductive when structured security evidence is already sufficient. We argue that malware explanation is primarily a signal-extraction task, not a knowledge-retrieval problem, and outline design recommendations for secure development workflows.

</details>


### [107] [A Validated Prompt Bank for Malicious Code Generation: Separating Executable Weapons from Security Knowledge in 1,554 Consensus-Labeled Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03179)
*Richard J. Young,Gregory D. Moody*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出现有恶意代码任务的拒绝基准混淆了"可执行恶意软件请求"与"有害安全知识请求"，并引入"武器vs知识"分类轴，通过五模型共识协议（涵盖Anthropic、OpenAI、Google、智谱、阿里五家）对3133条提示进行分类，得到1554条共识-CODE集和388条共识-KNOWLEDGE集，五裁判间Fleiss' kappa达0.876（几乎完全一致），全部提示均达到3/5多数阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将可执行恶意软件请求与有害安全知识请求混为一谈，而这两类请求可能触发安全对齐语言模型不同的拒绝路径，单一拒绝率统计无法区分两者，因此需要建立更精细的分类体系。

Method: 提出武器vs知识分类轴，采用五模型共识协议（Anthropic、OpenAI、Google、智谱、阿里五家LLM裁判），每个裁判对提示给出二元CODE/KNOWLEDGE标签，基于3/5多数规则确定共识，用Fleiss' kappa和bootstrap 95%置信区间量化裁判间信度。

Result: 在3133条提示上，五裁判Fleiss' kappa = 0.876 [95% CI: 0.862, 0.888]，达到"几乎完全一致"水平；69.3%的提示实现5/5一致同意；全部3133条提示均达到3/5阈值，零条被排除；产出1554条共识-CODE集和388条共识-KNOWLEDGE集。
