Conclusion: 武器vs知识分类轴可作为代码安全评估的组织轴心，本文贡献在于提供了信度经量化的数据集和分类框架，模型行为是否在该轴上有差异则由配套基准论文探究。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks of language-model refusal on malicious-coding tasks routinely conflate requests for executable malicious software with requests for harmful security knowledge. This conflation matters because the two request types plausibly trigger distinct refusal pathways in safety-aligned language models, and a single refusal-rate statistic computed over a mixture cannot isolate either. This paper introduces a weapons-versus-knowledge classification axis, operationalized through a five-model consensus protocol, and applies it to 3,133 prompts drawn from four public benchmarks, yielding a 1,554-prompt consensus-CODE bank (the primary released artifact) and a 388-prompt consensus-KNOWLEDGE comparison set used by the companion benchmark paper. The consensus pipeline uses five large-language-model judges spanning four vendor families (Anthropic, OpenAI, Google, Zhipu AI, Alibaba), each issuing a binary CODE/KNOWLEDGE label per prompt under a three-of-five majority rule, with inter-rater reliability quantified by Fleiss' kappa with bootstrap 95% confidence intervals. Across all 3,133 prompts the five judges achieve kappa = 0.876 [95% CI: 0.862, 0.888], "almost perfect" agreement by the Landis & Koch convention, with 69.3% of prompts unanimous at five-of-five; all 3,133 prompts reached the 3-of-5 threshold, so the consensus pipeline produced zero ambiguity-excluded prompts. Whether the axis separates model behavior in practice is an empirical question this paper leaves to the companion benchmark study; the present contribution is the reliability-documented artifact and the case for treating the weapons-versus-knowledge distinction as the organizing axis of code-safety evaluation.

</details>


### [108] [Dependency-Aware Privacy for Multi-turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03188)
*Divyam Anshumaan,Sarthak Choudhary,Nils Palumbo,Somesh Jha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RootGuard通过一次性对根属性加噪，后续所有推导值均基于加噪后的根值确定性计算，利用后处理定理使隐私预算不随交互轮次增加而退化，在NHANES医疗诊断模板上相比独立加噪实现2.3-3.0倍更低的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于度量差分隐私的提示净化器在每轮交互中独立处理发布数据，导致攻击者可通过跨轮组合攻击恢复隐私属性，隐私随每次发布而持续退化。这种退化在隐私属性为计算图根节点时尤为严重，独立加噪推导值会使根节点的区分性放大至推导函数的Lipschitz常数倍，远超名义隐私参数。

Method: RootGuard对根属性值进行一次加噪，后续所有发布值均基于已加噪的根值通过确定性函数计算得出，利用后处理定理保证隐私仅取决于初始根加噪。进一步利用结构领域知识（如BMI由身高体重推导、或已知目标函数）在多个根之间分配隐私预算，优化隐私-效用权衡。

Result: 在八个NHANES医疗诊断模板上，$\varepsilon=0.1$时RootGuard实现2.3-3.0倍更低的误差（7.6% vs 17.1% wMAPE）。在最坏情况攻击者迫使t轮交互时，独立加噪总预算$B=t\cdot\varepsilon$，RootGuard将更大预算分配到根上，而独立加噪每轮仅花费$\varepsilon$且攻击者获得t个观测值进行MAP重建，形成双重非对称性：更多轮次有利于RootGuard而削弱独立加噪。

Conclusion: RootGuard通过根值加噪+确定性推导从根本上解决了多轮交互中隐私持续退化的问题，MAP重建攻击下查询越多对独立加噪越不利，而RootGuard保持不变。

Abstract: LLM agents release private data across multi-service interactions. Existing prompt sanitizers based on metric differential privacy treat each release independently, so adversaries combining releases across turns can recover private attributes; privacy degrades with every release.
  This degradation is fundamental: when private attributes are the \emph{roots} of a computation graph, independently noising a derived value amplifies the root's distinguishability by up to the deriving function's Lipschitz constant $L$, which can far exceed the nominal privacy parameter for nonlinear functions in medical and financial workflows.
  RootGuard sanitizes root values once and computes subsequent releases deterministically from the noised roots. By the post-processing theorem, the privacy guarantee depends only on the initial root sanitization, regardless of the adversary's functions or number of turns, and derived values inherit privacy at zero marginal cost. RootGuard further exploits structural domain knowledge (e.g., BMI from height and weight, or a known target function) to allocate budget across roots, improving the privacy-utility tradeoff.
  A worst-case adversary forcing $t$ turns increases the total budget $B = t \cdot \varepsilon$. RootGuard distributes this larger budget across roots, while independent noising spends $\varepsilon$ per release and gives the adversary $t$ observations to combine via MAP reconstruction. This yields a \emph{double asymmetry}: more turns aid RootGuard while weakening independent noising.
  On eight NHANES medical diagnostic templates, RootGuard achieves $2.3$--$3.0\times$ lower target error than independent noising at $\varepsilon = 0.1$ (7.6\% vs.\ 17.1\% wMAPE at $B = (2k{+}1)\varepsilon$). Under MAP reconstruction, more queries strengthen attacks against independent noising while RootGuard remains invariant.

</details>


### [109] [When Agents Handle Secrets: A Survey of Confidential Computing for Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03213)
*Javad Forough,Marios Kogias,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了基于TEE（可信执行环境）的机密计算如何应对LLM驱动Agent系统面临的安全威胁（如提示注入、凭证窃取、上下文泄露等），提出了六类TEE平台分类、九项安全目标的威胁模型，并指出当前缺乏端到端的整体安全框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的Agent系统（规划、调用工具、持久记忆、MCP/A2A协议协作）引入了与传统模型推理截然不同的攻击面，现有纯软件防御可被具有足够权限的攻击者（如被攻破的云运营商）悄无声息地绕过，亟需硬件级安全保障。

Method: 以综述形式统一四部分分析：(i) 六类TEE平台分类（Intel SGX/TDX、AMD SEV-SNP、ARM TrustZone/CCA、NVIDIA H100 CC）及性能权衡；(ii) 以Agent为核心的威胁模型，覆盖感知、规划、记忆、行动、协调五层并映射九项安全目标；(iii) 对比现有CC防御中哪些可沿用自单次推理、哪些需Agent专属设计；(iv) 提出六项开放挑战（如多跳Agent链的复合认证、GPU-TEE在LLM规模下的性能）。

Result: 多个硬件信任基元已成熟到可用于特定部署场景，但当前不存在将TEE技术统一整合为生产级Agent AI安全基底的端到端框架。

Conclusion: 机密计算（CC）通过TEE和远程认证为Agent AI提供了硬件根信任的安全替代方案，能有效抵御特权软件攻击，但从碎片化TEE能力到可落地的整体安全架构仍有六大关键挑战亟待解决。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems, specifically LLM-driven agents that plan, invoke tools, maintain persistent memory, and delegate tasks to peer agents via protocols such as MCP and A2A, introduce a threat surface that differs materially from standalone model inference. Agents accumulate sensitive context, hold credentials, and operate across pipelines no single party fully controls, enabling prompt injection, context exfiltration, credential theft, and inter-agent message poisoning. Current defenses operate entirely within the software stack and can be silently bypassed by a sufficiently privileged adversary such as a compromised cloud operator. Confidential computing (CC) offers a hardware-rooted alternative: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) isolate agent code and data from privileged system software, while remote attestation enables verifiable trust across distributed deployments. This survey synthesizes the design space in four parts: (i) a unified taxonomy of six TEE platforms (Intel SGX, Intel TDX, AMD SEV-SNP, ARM TrustZone, ARM CCA, and NVIDIA H100 CC) covering deployment roles and performance tradeoffs; (ii) an agent-centric threat model spanning perception, planning, memory, action, and coordination layers mapped to nine security goals; (iii) a comparative survey of CC-based defenses distinguishing findings that transfer from single-call inference versus what requires new agentic designs; and (iv) six open challenges including compound attestation for multi-hop agent chains and GPU-TEE performance at LLM scale. While several hardware trust primitives appear mature enough for targeted deployments, no broadly established end-to-end framework yet binds them into a coherent security substrate for production agentic AI.

</details>


### [110] [MAGE: Safeguarding LLM Agents against Long-Horizon Threats via Shadow Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03228)
*Yuhui Wang,Tanqiu Jiang,Jiacheng Liang,Charles Fleming,Ting Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MAGE是一种受系统安全中"影子栈"启发的防御框架，通过维护专用的安全导向智能体记忆来检测和缓解针对LLM智能体的长视界威胁，在检测精度、早期检测能力方面显著优于现有方法，且对智能体效用影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM驱动的智能体被部署于复杂真实任务，它们面临一类利用扩展的用户-智能体-环境交互来追求恶意目标的新型攻击（长视界威胁），这些威胁在单轮对话中不可能实现，对智能体的安全部署构成重大风险。

Method: MAGE框架受系统安全中"影子栈"抽象启发，维护一个专用的、面向安全的智能体记忆，该记忆在智能体完整执行轨迹中提取并保留安全关键上下文，利用这种影子内存在待执行动作执行前主动评估其风险。

Result: 大量评估表明，MAGE在检测精度上显著优于现有防御方法，能对大多数攻击实现早期阶段检测，且对智能体效用引入的开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，MAGE是首个利用智能体记忆方法检测和缓解长视界威胁的框架，为这一关键挑战建立了新范式，并为未来研究开辟了有前景的方向。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM)-powered agents are increasingly deployed to perform complex, real-world tasks, they face a growing class of attacks that exploit extended user-agent-environment interactions to pursue malicious objectives improbable in single-turn settings. Such long-horizon threats pose significant risks to the safe deployment of LLM agents in critical domains. In this paper, we present MAGE (Memory As Guardrail Enforcement), a novel defensive framework designed to counter a wide range of long-horizon threats. Inspired by the "shadow stack" abstraction in systems security, MAGE maintains a dedicated, safety-focused agentic memory that distills and retains safety-critical context across the agent's full execution trajectory, leveraging this shadow memory to proactively assess the risk of pending actions prior to their execution. Extensive evaluation demonstrates that MAGE substantially outperforms existing defenses across diverse long-horizon threats in detection accuracy, achieves early-stage detection for the majority of attacks, and introduces only negligible overhead to agent utility. To our best knowledge, MAGE represents the first framework to detect and mitigate long-horizon threats using an agentic memory approach, establishing a new paradigm for this critical challenge and opening promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [111] [SILMARILS: Information-Theoretic and Quantum-Secure Designated-Verifier Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03230)
*Hassan Khodaiemehr,Khadijeh Bagheri,Chen Feng,Dariia Porechna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SILMARILS 是一个基于有限域 𝔽ₚ 上最小代数核心、利用真随机性和完美 2-out-of-2 Shamir 秘密共享构建的签名框架。它支持两方和三方模式：两方模式下实现可转移指定验证者（TDV）签名，指定验证者可模拟不可区分的接受会话，并支持凭据 r 进行公开验证，但外部者无法区分签名与模拟；三方模式下采用 Fitzi et al. 广播模型，获得统计安全的签名协议。该方案在 ROM 和 QROM 中均安全，密钥和签名尺寸远小于 Dilithium、Falcon、SPHINCS⁺ 等标准化后量子方案，适用于区块链场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后量子签名方案（如 Dilithium、Falcon、SPHINCS⁺）在区块链等需要指定验证者签名（DV）和可转移性的场景中存在效率或功能不足，缺乏同时支持两方 TDV 签名与三方统计安全签名、且具备后量子安全性的统一代数框架。

Method: 基于 𝔽ₚ 上的最小代数核心，使用真随机性与完美 2-out-of-2 Shamir 秘密共享构建签名框架。两方模式实现 TDV 签名（满足 Jakobsson-Sako-Impagliazzo DV 安全性），指定验证者可模拟不可区分会话并生成公开验证凭据 r；三方模式采用 Fitzi et al. 广播模型，实现统计安全的签名协议。安全性分析覆盖 Pure IT、IT+ROM 和 QROM 模型，并将 Fitzi 框架扩展到经典 I/O 的量子敌手。

Result: 两方模式下实现了 EUF-CMA^¬DV 安全性（对所有非指定验证者），三方模式下实现了基于模拟的统计安全性（错误率 1/p）。密钥和签名尺寸显著小于 Dilithium、Falcon、SPHINCS⁺ 等标准化后量子方案，提供后量子 TDV 签名能力。

Conclusion: SILMARILS 提供了一个简洁、高效且功能丰富的后量子签名框架，特别适合需要指定验证者、可转移性和公开验证的区块链应用，同时其正确性、保密性、可转移性和不可伪造性等价于基于模拟的安全性。

Abstract: SILMARILS is built from a minimal algebraic core over $\mathbb{F}_p$ using true randomness and perfect $2$-out-of-$2$ Shamir secret sharing. The framework supports both two-party and three-party modes. In the two-party setting, SILMARILS realizes a transferable designated-verifier (TDV) signature scheme. The designated verifier can simulate accepting transcripts indistinguishable from real ones, achieving Jakobsson-Sako-Impagliazzo DV security. The verifier may publish a receipt $r$ enabling public verification, yet even with $r$, no external party can tell whether a transcript was signed or simulated. As DV signatures permit simulation, standard EUF-CMA cannot hold for the designated verifier; instead, we prove $\mathsf{EUF\text{-}CMA}^{\neg\mathsf{DV}}$ security for all non-designated verifiers in both the random oracle model (ROM) and quantum random oracle model (QROM). In the three-party mode, adopting the broadcast model of Fitzi et al., we obtain a statistically secure signature protocol with simulation-based security and error~$1/p$. We analyze security in the Pure IT model, the IT+ROM, and the QROM, extending the Fitzi et al. framework to quantum adversaries with classical I/O. Correctness, secrecy, transferability, and unforgeability for non-designated parties remain equivalent to simulation-based security. Thanks to its simple algebraic structure, SILMARILS achieves substantially smaller keys and signatures than standardized post-quantum schemes such as Dilithium, Falcon, and SPHINCS$^+$, while providing post-quantum security in a TDV setting well suited to blockchain applications.

</details>


### [112] [Cryptographic Registry Provenance: Structural Defense Against Dependency Confusion in AI Package Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03309)
*Alan L. McCann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套密码学分发溯源系统，通过注册表身份签名、双签名模型和权威命名空间绑定三层防御机制，解决软件依赖混淆攻击问题。跨八个生态系统的比较显示，目前尚无生态同时具备强制性发布者签名、注册表密码学身份、注册表反签名和消费者端密码学强制验证。系统还扩展到AI生成溯源和治理强制依赖解析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖混淆攻击利用软件分发中的结构性缺陷——软件包安装后无法通过密码学证明其来自哪个注册表。现有防御均为基于配置的方案，配置错误时会静默失效，存在严重安全隐患。

Method: 提出三层密码学分发溯源系统：(1) 密码学注册表身份——每个注册表持有Ed25519密钥对并对分发的每件制品签名；(2) 双签名模型——发布者在打包时签名，注册表在发布时反签名；(3) 权威命名空间绑定——消费者固定注册表指纹，解析器从密码学上拒绝来自未授权注册表的制品。

Result: 在八个生态系统（npm、Cargo、Hex.pm、PyPI、Go模块、Docker/OCI、NuGet、Maven）的横向比较中，目前没有任何生态系统同时具备强制性发布者签名、密码学注册表身份、强制性注册表反签名和消费者端密码学强制验证四项能力。系统可扩展到AI生成溯源，并通过与三层运行时治理架构集成，形成无密码学断点的四阶段生命周期链。

Conclusion: 该密码学分发溯源系统通过三层防御机制有效防范依赖混淆攻击，要求攻击者同时攻破三层才能成功。系统可扩展性强，能覆盖AI生成溯源和治理强制依赖解析场景，填补了现有软件供应链安全中的密码学断层。

Abstract: Dependency confusion attacks exploit a structural gap in software distribution: once a package is installed, there is no cryptographic proof of which registry distributed it. Every existing defense is configuration-based and fails silently when misconfigured. We present a cryptographic distribution provenance system comprising three components: (1) cryptographic registry identity, where every registry holds an Ed25519 keypair and signs every artifact it distributes; (2) a dual-signature model, where the publisher signs at packaging time and the registry countersigns at publication time; and (3) authoritative namespace binding, where consumers pin registry fingerprints and the resolver cryptographically rejects artifacts from unauthorized registries. These create three defense layers requiring simultaneous compromise for a successful attack. A comparison across eight ecosystems (npm, Cargo, Hex.pm, PyPI, Go modules, Docker/OCI, NuGet, Maven) shows no existing ecosystem combines mandatory publisher signing, cryptographic registry identity, mandatory registry countersigning, and consumer-side cryptographic enforcement. The system extends to AI-generation provenance as a signed attribute and governance-enforced dependency resolution. A case study integrates distribution provenance with a three-layer runtime governance architecture, creating a four-phase lifecycle chain with no cryptographic gaps.

</details>


### [113] [ARGUS: Defending LLM Agents Against Context-Aware Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03378)
*Shihao Weng,Yang Feng,Jinrui Zhang,Xiaofei Xie,Jiongchi Yu,Jia Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentLure 基准测试，用于评估上下文感知的提示注入攻击对 LLM 智能体的威胁，并提出 ARGUS 防御机制——通过构建影响溯源图追踪不可信上下文传播，在保留 87.5% 任务效用的同时将攻击成功率降至 3.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对 LLM 智能体的提示注入攻击基准和防御方法均假设上下文无关的场景（智能体仅依赖用户指令，攻击简单且独立于上下文），无法捕捉真实部署中智能体行为依赖动态上下文、攻击者可自适应调整攻击的现实情况，存在严重安全漏洞。

Method: 提出 AgentLure 基准，覆盖四个智能体领域和八种攻击向量；提出 ARGUS 防御机制，构建影响溯源图（influence provenance graph）追踪不可信上下文在智能体决策中的传播路径，在执行前验证决策是否基于可信证据。

Result: 现有防御在 AgentLure 设置下表现不佳；ARGUS 将攻击成功率降至 3.8%，同时保持 87.5% 的任务效用，显著优于现有防御，并对自适应白盒攻击具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 上下文感知的提示注入攻击对 LLM 智能体构成严重威胁，现有防御存在根本性局限；基于溯源感知决策审计的 ARGUS 方法是有效的应对方案。

Abstract: The rise of Large Language Model (LLM) agents, augmented with tool use, skills, and external knowledge, has introduced new security risks. Among them, prompt injection attacks, where adversaries embed malicious instructions into the agent workflow, have emerged as the primary threat. However, existing benchmarks and defenses are fundamentally limited as they assume context-insensitive settings in which the agent works under a fully specified user instruction, and the attacks are straightforward and context-independent. As a result, they fail to capture real-world deployments where agent behavior usually depends on dynamic context, not just the user prompt, and adversaries can adapt their attacks to different context. Similarly, existing defenses built on this narrow threat model overlook the nature of real-world agent delegation.
  In this paper, we present AgentLure, a benchmark that captures context-dependent tasks and context-aware prompt injection attacks. AgentLure spans four agentic domains and eight attack vectors across diverse attack surfaces. Our evaluation shows that existing defenses often struggle in this setting, yielding poor performance against such attacks in agentic systems. To address this limitation, we propose ARGUS, a defense mechanism that enforces provenance-aware decision auditing for LLM agents. ARGUS constructs an influence provenance graph to track how untrusted context propagates into agent decisions and verify whether a decision is justified by trustworthy evidence before execution. Our evaluation shows ARGUS reduces attack success rate to 3.8% while preserving 87.5% task utility, significantly outperforming existing defenses and remaining robust against adaptive white-box adversaries.

</details>


### [114] [DECKER: Domain-invariant Embedding for Cross-Keyboard Extraction and Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03384)
*Bikrant Bikram Pratap Maurya,Nitin Choudhury,Daksh Agarwal,Arun Balaji Buduru*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出HEAR数据集（53人、37种笔记本键盘、3种场景）和DECKER框架（四阶段域不变击键推理），在跨键盘、跨用户、噪声环境下显著提升击键识别准确率，证明了声学侧信道攻击的实际安全威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有声学侧信道攻击研究受限于小规模数据集，缺乏用户、键盘、环境的多样性，难以系统评估跨设备、跨麦克风和噪声条件下的攻击泛化能力。

Method: 构建HEAR数据集（53参与者×37键盘×3场景）；提出DECKER框架，包含键盘特征归一化、域对抗解耦、跨键盘对比对齐、声学风格随机化四阶段，并引入LLM后处理进行句子级修正。

Result: 在HEAR基准上，DECKER在跨键盘和跨用户设置中显著优于强基线，LLM后处理进一步提升了击键序列的识别准确率。

Conclusion: 声学侧信道攻击在不同用户、设备和噪声环境下仍然有效，具有实际安全风险，需要引起重视。

Abstract: Acoustic side-channel attacks (ASCA) on keyboards pose a significant security risk, as keystrokes can be inferred from typing acoustics, revealing sensitive information. Prior ASCA studies are limited by small-scale datasets with restricted diversity in users, keyboards, and environments, constraining analysis across devices, microphones, and noise conditions. We introduce HEAR, a dataset designed to study ASCA along three axes: keyboard generalization, noise adaptation, and user bias. HEAR contains recordings from 53 participants using 37 laptop keyboards, collected in three realistic settings: (1) external microphone capture, (2) device microphone capture without network noise, and (3) VoIP-based streaming capture. This enables controlled evaluation across users, keyboards, and environments. On HEAR, we establish an ASCA benchmark spanning conventional features and pre-trained representations from raw audio and spectrograms in unimodal and multimodal settings. We propose DECKER, a domain-invariant keystroke inference framework with four stages: (1) Keyboard Signature Normalization to reduce device coloration, (2) domain-adversarial disentanglement to suppress keyboard identity, (3) supervised cross-keyboard contrastive alignment to enforce key consistency, and (4) Acoustic Style Randomization to synthesize unseen keyboard responses. We further explore sentence-level inference using an LLM-based post-processing layer to refine keystroke sequences via linguistic context. Results on HEAR show DECKER improves keystroke identification over strong baselines, particularly in cross-keyboard and cross-user settings, with further gains from language-model rectification. These findings highlight that ASCA remains effective across diverse users, devices, and noisy environments, underscoring its practical security risk.

</details>


### [115] [Exposing LLM Safety Gaps Through Mathematical Encoding:New Attacks and Systematic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03441)
*Haoyu Zhang,Mohammad Zandsalimy,Shanu Sushmita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 将有害提示编码为连贯的数学问题（如集合论、形式逻辑、量子力学）可绕过LLM安全机制，在8个目标模型上达到46%-56%的平均攻击成功率；攻击有效性取决于对有害内容的深度数学化重构，而非表面数学符号；较新模型（GPT-5系列）表现出更强鲁棒性但仍存在漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示当前LLM安全机制（主要依赖语义模式匹配）在面对数学形式化编码的有害提示时存在根本性缺陷，推动安全框架从表面语义分析向数学结构推理进化。

Method: 将有害提示编码为集合论、形式逻辑、量子力学等数学形式体系的连贯问题，在8个目标模型、2个基准数据集上测试攻击成功率；对比基于规则的数学格式化编码与深度数学化重构的效果差异；引入新提出的形式逻辑编码方法；通过重复后处理验证攻击鲁棒性。

Result: 数学编码攻击在8个模型上达到46%-56%平均成功率；基于规则的数学格式化编码效果与未编码基线无显著差异；形式逻辑编码取得与集合论编码相当的攻击成功率；攻击对重复后处理具有鲁棒性；GPT-5系列等新模型鲁棒性明显优于旧模型但仍有漏洞。

Conclusion: 当前基于语义模式匹配的安全框架无法应对数学形式化的有害内容，需要发展能够推理数学结构而非表层语义的新型防御机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) employ safety mechanisms to prevent harmful outputs, yet these defenses primarily rely on semantic pattern matching. We show that encoding harmful prompts as coherent mathematical problems -- using formalisms such as set theory, formal logic, and quantum mechanics -- bypasses these filters at high rates, achieving 46%--56% average attack success across eight target models and two established benchmarks. Crucially, the effectiveness depends not on mathematical notation itself, but on whether a helper LLM deeply reformulates the harmful content into a genuine mathematical problem: rule-based encodings that apply mathematical formatting without such reformulation perform no better than unencoded baselines. We introduce a novel Formal Logic encoding that achieves attack success comparable to Set Theory, demonstrating that this vulnerability generalizes across mathematical formalisms. Additional experiments with repeat post-processing confirm that these attacks are robust to simple prompt augmentation. Notably, newer models (GPT-5, GPT-5-Mini) show substantially greater robustness than older models, though they remain vulnerable. Our findings highlight fundamental gaps in current safety frameworks and motivate defenses that reason about mathematical structure rather than surface-level semantics.

</details>


### [116] [ZK-Value: A Practical Zero-Knowledge System for Verifiable Data Valuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03581)
*Zhaoyu Wang,Pingchuan Ma,Zhantong Xue,Yuguang Zhou,Qixin Zhang,Xiaoqin Zhang,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ZK-Value是一个实用的端到端零知识数据估值系统，通过LSH-Shapley估值原语、ZK-LSH-Shapley协议和结构性证明系统优化，在保证隐私和可验证性的同时，实现了可扩展的高效数据估值，证明时间比现有ZK基线快12.6-68.1倍，验证时间低于4.6秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据市场在Shapley值估值中面临隐私性与可验证性之间的冲突：市场运营商单独运行估值过程，参与者和外部审计员无法在不访问底层私有数据的情况下独立重新计算评分。虽然零知识证明理论上可以调和这一矛盾，但现有ZK估值系统因证明时间过长或需要披露验证群体而无法扩展到真实数据市场的需求。

Method: 提出ZK-Value系统，采用三层协同设计：(1) LSH-Shapley：基于局部敏感哈希的估值原语，用每个桶的碰撞计数替代昂贵的成对距离度量；(2) ZK-LSH-Shapley：定制化ZKP协议，通过将碰撞计数编码为桶级直方图而非朴素的逐对张量，大幅减少见证大小；(3) 结构性证明系统优化：超级预言机批处理和稀疏性跳过技术。

Result: 在12个标准数据集上评估，ZK-Value的估值质量与最先进基线相当（与精确KNN-Shapley的AUROC差异在0.033以内），证明时间在秒到分钟级别，比专门的ZK基线快12.6-68.1倍，验证时间低于4.6秒。

Conclusion: ZK-Value通过全协同设计的架构成功弥合了ZK数据估值在隐私、可验证性和可扩展性之间的鸿沟，为实际数据市场提供了实用、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Data valuation is a foundational task in data marketplaces, where a Shapley-value attribution determines how a buyer's payment is distributed among data providers. Typically, the marketplace operator runs this attribution alone, requiring participants and external auditors to trust scores they cannot independently recompute on the underlying private data. While zero-knowledge proofs (ZKPs) can theoretically reconcile this conflict between privacy and verifiability, existing ZK valuation systems fail to scale to real-world marketplace demands due to prohibitive proving times or the requirement to disclose validation cohorts.
  We present ZK-Value, a practical, end-to-end ZK data-valuation system. Our solution bridges the scalability gap through a fully co-designed architecture: (1) LSH-Shapley, a locality-based valuation primitive that replaces expensive pairwise distance metrics with per-bucket collision counts; (2) ZK-LSH-Shapley, a tailored ZKP protocol that drastically reduces witness size by encoding these counts into bucket-level histograms rather than naive per-pair tensors; and (3) structural proof-system optimizations, specifically super-oracle batching and sparsity skipping. Evaluated across 12 standard datasets, ZK-Value delivers valuation quality on par with state-of-the-art baselines (within 0.033 AUROC of exact KNN-Shapley), while generating proofs in seconds to minutes and outperforming specialized ZK baselines by 12.6x to 68.1x in proving time, with verification in under 4.6 s.

</details>


### [117] [The Infinite Mutation Engine? Measuring Polymorphism in LLM-Generated Offensive Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03619)
*Gabriel Hortea,Juan Tapiador*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文测量了商业LLM（Claude Opus 4.6）作为自动化恶意软件生成器的多态能力，通过双智能体四阶段流水线生成数据窃取载荷，发现无需显式要求多态时结构距离高而语义距离低，显式提示可大幅增强结构多样性且保持正确性，仅以少量token和API成本为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设LLM能产生足够多态的载荷，但未量化同一载荷重复构建时的变异程度，或模型在被告知避免先前实现时的变异程度。

Method: 构建双智能体（代码生成器+测试器）、四阶段（生成、测试、优化）流水线，生成包含文件遍历、加密、外泄、集成的数据窃取载荷。在两种设置下生成载荷：仅指定功能需求的提示，以及注入结构化先验结果历史以强制分化的提示。沿结构（AST）和语义（嵌入）轴测量成对距离。

Result: 无需显式多态时结构距离高、语义距离低；显式提示大幅增强结构多样性且保持正确性；代价为约5倍token增加，LLM调用从4.2增至4.5次，API成本分别为0.41美元和0.73美元。

Conclusion: 单一商业LLM可以廉价生成大量行为等价但结构多样的载荷，有助于规避基于签名的检测和基于相似性的聚类。

Abstract: Malware authors have traditionally relied on polymorphic techniques to produce variants in the same malware family, complicating signature-based detection. Integrating generative AI into offensive toolchains enables attackers to synthesize structurally diverse payloads with identical behavior, raising the question of how much polymorphism LLMs provide. Recent work has assumed that LLMs can produce sufficiently polymorphic payloads, leaving unquantified the variation that emerges when an attacker repeatedly builds the same payload, or explicitly instructs the model to avoid prior implementations. In this work, we measure the polymorphic capacity of a commercial model (Claude Opus 4.6) as an automated malware generator. We build a dual-agent, four-stage pipeline that generates, tests, and refines a data-exfiltration payload comprising file traversal, encryption, exfiltration, and integration. We produce payloads in two settings: using prompts that specify only functional requirements, and using prompts that inject a structured history of prior outcomes to force divergence. We measure pairwise distances along structural (AST) and semantic (embedding) axes, finding that when polymorphism is not explicitly required, structural distances are high while semantic distances remain low; i.e., implementations diverge widely without changing high-level behavior. Explicit prompting substantially amplifies this structural diversity while preserving correctness, at the cost of roughly 5 times more tokens but only a small increase in LLM calls (from $4.2$ to $4.5$ per payload, with effective API costs of \$0.41 and \$0.73). These results show that a single commercial LLM can cheaply generate large populations of behaviorally equivalent yet structurally diverse payloads, facilitating the evasion of signature-based detection rules and similarity-based clustering.

</details>


### [118] [Tailored Prompts, Targeted Protection: Vulnerability-Specific LLM Analysis for Smart Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03697)
*Xing Zhang,Keyu Zhang,Taohong Zhu,Anbang Ruan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的智能合约漏洞检测框架，利用精确的AST上下文提取和漏洞特定提示设计，在15个区块链平台的3,200+真实项目中构建了31,165个专业标注的漏洞实例数据集，针对13种漏洞类型实现平均正例召回率0.92、负例召回率0.85。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能合约漏洞检测方法缺乏跨漏洞类型的灵活性，且严重依赖人工制定的专家规则，难以应对区块链上多样化的安全漏洞。

Method: 提出LLM-based框架，包括：(1)构建大规模专业标注数据集（31,165个样本，覆盖15个区块链平台）；(2)基于AST的精确上下文提取；(3)漏洞特定的提示设计；(4)为13种常见漏洞类别实例化定制检测器。

Result: 平均正例召回率0.92，平均负例召回率0.85，证明了精心设计的上下文提示在可扩展、高精度智能合约安全分析中的潜力。

Conclusion: 精心设计的上下文提示工程能够实现可扩展且高精度的智能合约安全分析，LLM在漏洞检测领域具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Smart contracts on blockchains are prone to diverse security vulnerabilities that can lead to significant financial losses due to their immutable nature. Existing detection approaches often lack flexibility across vulnerability types and rely heavily on manually crafted expert rules. In this paper, we present an LLM-based framework for practical smart contract vulnerability detection. We construct and release a large-scale dataset comprising 31,165 professionally annotated vulnerability instances collected from over 3,200 real-world projects across 15 major blockchain platforms. Our approach leverages precise AST-based context extraction and vulnerability-specific prompt design to instantiate customized detectors for 13 prevalent vulnerability categories. Experimental results demonstrate strong effectiveness, achieving an average positive recall of 0.92 and an average negative recall of 0.85, highlighting the potential of carefully engineered contextual prompting for scalable and high-precision smart contract security analysis.

</details>


### [119] [Internet of Things Security: A Survey on Common Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03744)
*Dalton Cézane Gomes Valadares,Luiz Antonio Pereira Silva,Daniel Hindemburg de Miranda Marques,Álvaro Alvares de Carvalho César Sobrinho,Andson Marreiros Balieiro,Mohamed Ahmed Hail,Mohammed B. Alshawki,Kyller Costa Gorgônio*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对IoT威胁态势进行了全面调查，描述了28种常见攻击，使用STRIDE模型和CVSS框架进行威胁分类与定量评估，建立了威胁与五大漏洞类别的映射关系，并讨论了现有缓解技术及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT设备的指数级增长带来了严峻的安全挑战，设备计算能力有限、缺乏标准化且攻击面不断扩大，安全是确保IoT环境信任度的关键前提，亟需系统性地理解和应对这些威胁。

Method: 采用STRIDE模型对威胁进行功能分类，结合CVSS框架进行定量严重性评估，并将28种常见攻击映射到五大漏洞类别（过程、代码、通信、操作、设备），从而结构化地分析IoT威胁全景。

Result: 识别并描述了28种常见IoT攻击，建立了威胁与五类漏洞的映射关系，揭示了攻击者利用的具体技术切入点，并汇总了现有的缓解技术及新兴研究范式。

Conclusion: 本文为研究者和实践者提供了系统化的IoT安全技术基础，指明了未来研究方向和研究空白，旨在帮助构建更具弹性和安全性的IoT生态系统。

Abstract: The exponential growth of the Internet of Things (IoT) has integrated connected devices into various sectors like smart cities, digital health, and Industry 4.0, generating vast amounts of real-time data to support intelligent decision-making. However, this widespread adoption is fundamentally challenged by significant security risks, primarily due to the inherent computational limitations of devices, lack of standardization, and an expanding attack surface. Given that security is paramount to ensuring trust in these environments, this paper presents a comprehensive survey and a multi-dimensional analysis of the IoT threat landscape. It describes 28 common attacks, ranging from traditional threats, such as Man-in-the-Middle, to specialized IoT exploits, including node replication and skimming. To provide a structured understanding of these risks, we employ the STRIDE model for functional threat classification alongside the CVSS framework for quantitative criticality assessment. Furthermore, the research establishes a robust mapping between these threats and five foundational vulnerability classes (Process, Code, Communication, Operation, and Device), uncovering the specific technical entry points exploited by adversaries. Beyond threat identification, the survey presents state-of-the-art mitigation techniques and discusses emerging paradigms and research gaps, working as a roadmap for future investigation and providing a consolidated technical foundation for both researchers and practitioners aiming to build resilient and secure IoT ecosystems.

</details>


### [120] [GPUBreach: Privilege Escalation Attacks on GPUs using Rowhammer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03812)
*Chris S. Lin,Yuqin Yan,Guozhen Ding,Joyce Qu,Joseph Zhu,David Lie,Gururaj Saileshwar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文展示了GPU上的Rowhammer攻击可以通过操控页表实现进程间内存访问，进而实现权限提升，包括窃取加密密钥、篡改GPU汇编代码，甚至突破IOMMU防护实现CPU端提权获取系统控制权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前GPU上的Rowhammer攻击仅限于注入非定向比特翻转以降低ML模型精度，尚未实现类似CPU的权限提升攻击。本文旨在证明GPU Rowhammer攻击可以达到与CPU攻击同等的威力。

Method: 利用GPU页表管理机制，识别新页表分配的位置和时间点，使非特权用户CUDA内核通过Rowhammer比特翻转定向篡改驻留在GPU内存中的页表，从而获得跨进程/共租户的GPU内存访问权限。

Result: 首次实现了GPU侧的权限提升攻击，能够泄露cuPQC库中的加密密钥，篡改模型GPU汇编代码以实现更隐蔽的模型降级。进一步证明了GPU提权可导致CPU侧提权，突破IOMMU防护，使恶意用户程序获取root shell和系统级控制权。

Conclusion: GPU Rowhammer攻击的威力可与CPU攻击相媲美，且GPU侧权限提升可级联至CPU侧，对系统安全构成严重威胁。

Abstract: NVIDIA GPUs with GDDR memories have been shown susceptible to Rowhammer-based bit-flips, similar to CPUs. However, Rowhammer exploits on GPUs have been limited to injecting untargeted bit-flips in victim data like weights of machine learning models, to degrade model accuracy, unlike CPU exploits shown capable of privilege escalation. In this paper, we demonstrate that GPU Rowhammer exploits can be as potent as CPU Rowhammer attacks. By exploiting the GPU page table management to identify when and where new page tables are allocated, we enable an unprivileged user CUDA kernel of one process to use RowHammer bit-flips to gain access to the GPU memory of other processes or co-tenants via targeted tampering of such page-tables resident on the GPU memory. Using this newly found primitive, we demonstrate the first GPU-side privilege escalation attacks, leaking secret data such as cryptographic keys from cuPQC libraries, and even tampering with the model's GPU assembly code to degrade models more stealthily than previous attacks. We further demonstrate that GPU-side privilege escalation can lead to CPU-side privilege escalation, defeating the protections provided by the IOMMU, enabling a malicious user-level program with GPU access to gain root shell and system-wide control, even in a non-multi-tenant setting.

</details>


### [121] [MOSAIC-Bench: Measuring Compositional Vulnerability Induction in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03952)
*Jonathan Steinberg,Oren Gal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MOSAIC-Bench基准测试，揭示当前编码智能体在面对由无害子任务组合成的恶意攻击链时，安全对齐机制失效的问题。实验表明9个生产级编码智能体在攻击链下ASR高达53-86%，而直接提示下仅0-20.4%；代码审查智能体对25.8%的含漏洞代码批准通过；将审查者重构为对抗性渗透测试者可显著降低规避率至3.0-17.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐仅评估孤立请求，忽视了模型在按序执行看似无害的子任务时，可能组合出恶意最终状态的结构性漏洞。编码智能体将任务分解为常规工单后，尽管每个工单通过安全审查，最终仍可能产出可被利用的代码。

Method: 构建了包含199个三阶段攻击链的MOSAIC-Bench基准，部署在10个Web应用、31个CWE类和5种编程语言上。设计了双轴评估体系：漏洞利用真实性与下游审查协议。对9个生产级编码智能体进行攻击链测试与直接提示对照实验，并评估代码审查智能体的批准率及对抗性渗透测试重构的效果。

Result: 1) 9个生产级编码智能体在攻击链下ASR为53-86%，仅2次拒绝；2) 直接提示下脆弱输出率降至0-20.4%（Claude主要拒绝，Codex主要加固）；3) 代码审查智能体批准25.8%的含漏洞累积diff；4) 完整上下文协议仅缩小50%的差距；5) 对抗性渗透测试框架下，审查者检测率达88.4%，误报率4.6%。

Conclusion: 任务分阶段编排使编码智能体同时丧失拒绝和加固两种防御模式，上下文碎片化不是唯一原因。将审查者重构为对抗性渗透测试者是一种可部署的非自适应缓解措施，能显著降低规避率。

Abstract: Coding agents often pass per-prompt safety review yet ship exploitable code when their tasks are decomposed into routine engineering tickets. The challenge is structural: existing safety alignment evaluates overt requests in isolation, leaving models blind to malicious end-states that emerge from sequenced compliance with innocuous-looking requests. We introduce MOSAIC-Bench (Malicious Objectives Sequenced As Innocuous Compliance), a benchmark of 199 three-stage attack chains paired with deterministic exploit oracles on deployed software substrates (10 web-application substrates, 31 CWE classes, 5 programming languages) that treats both exploit ground truth and downstream reviewer protocol as first-class evaluation axes. On this benchmark, nine production coding agents from Anthropic, OpenAI, Google, Moonshot, Zhipu, and Minimax compose innocuous tickets at 53-86% end-to-end ASR with only two refusals across all staged runs. In a matched direct-prompt experiment over four frontier Claude/Codex agents, vulnerable-output rates fall to 0-20.4%: Claude primarily refuses, while Codex primarily hardens rather than emitting the vulnerable implementation - ticket staging silences both defense modes simultaneously. Downstream, code reviewer agents approve 25.8% of these confirmed-vulnerable cumulative diffs as routine PRs, and a full-context implementation protocol closes only 50% of the staged/direct gap, ruling out context fragmentation as the sole explanation. As a deployable but non-adaptive mitigation, reframing the reviewer as an adversarial pentester reduces evasion across the evaluated reviewer subset; pentester framed evasion ranges from 3.0% to 17.6%, and an open-weight Gemma-4-E4B-it reviewer under this framing detects 88.4% of attacks on the dataset with a 4.6% false-positive rate measured on 608 real-world GitHub PRs.

</details>


### [122] [LIPPEN: A Lightweight In-Place Pointer Encryption Architecture for Pointer Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03974)
*Erfan Iravani,Lalit Prasad Peri,Mohannad Ismail,Charitha Tumkur Siddalingaradhya,Changwoo Min,Elif Bilge Kavun,Wenjie Xiong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LIPPEN 是一种硬件-软件协同设计的全指针加密方案，利用整个64位指针字段进行加密，实现了零元数据开销的强指针完整性和机密性保护，在FPGA原型上验证其性能开销与PAC方案相当，而面积和功耗开销可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的硬件内存安全防御方案存在不足：基于地址空间布局随机化（ASLR）的方案安全性有限；外部元数据方案虽提供强保证但引入额外内存访问和空间开销；就地认证机制（如ARM PAC）虽然开销低，但存在熵值有限、易受暴力破解和重用攻击的弱点。

Method: LIPPEN 将每个指针视为加密块，将指针与其执行上下文进行密码学绑定，在解引用时透明解密。通过重新利用整个64位指针字段进行加密（而非保留原始地址位），最大化熵值，消除截断认证码的暴力破解弱点，并保持与现有PAC使能软件的二进制兼容性。在FPGA上使用64位RISC-V Rocket和BOOM内核进行原型实现。

Result: 在微基准测试、nbench和SPEC CPU2017上的评估显示，LIPPEN提供了全面的指针保护，运行时开销与基于PAC的方案相当，面积和功耗开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: LIPPEN是在实际处理器中部署强指针保护的一个实用设计方案。

Abstract: Memory-safety violations in C and C++ programs continue to enable sophisticated exploitation techniques such as control-flow hijacking and data-oriented attacks. Existing hardware defenses either rely on address space layout randomization (ASLR) or attach explicit metadata to pointers to verify their integrity. External metadata schemes provide strong guarantees, but incur additional memory accesses and memory footprint overhead. In-place authentication mechanisms, such as ARM Pointer Authentication (PAC), achieve low overhead at the cost of limited entropy and susceptibility to brute-force and reuse attacks. This paper presents LIPPEN, a hardware-software co-design for full-pointer encryption that provides strong pointer integrity and confidentiality with zero metadata overhead. LIPPEN treats every pointer as an encrypted block, cryptographically binding it to its execution context and decrypting it transparently at dereference time. By re-purposing the entire 64-bit pointer field for encryption rather than preserving raw address bits, LIPPEN maximizes entropy, eliminates the brute-force weaknesses of truncated authentication codes, and maintains binary compatibility with existing PAC-enabled software. We prototype LIPPEN on FPGA using 64-bit RISC-V Rocket and BOOM cores, and evaluate it with microbenchmarks, nbench, and SPEC CPU2017. We compare against both an in-house RISC-V PAC implementation and Apple's PAC on the M1 processor. Across these workloads, LIPPEN provides comprehensive pointer protection with runtime overhead comparable to PAC-based schemes, while incurring negligible area and power overhead. These results show that LIPPEN is a practical design point for deploying strong pointer protection in real processors.

</details>


### [123] [Probabilistic-bit Guided CDCL for SAT Solving using Ising Consensus Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04033)
*Melki Bino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合SAT求解框架，将概率比特（p-bit）Ising采样器与CDCL求解器结合，通过将高一致性文字作为临时假设传递给CDCL来减少内部搜索开销。在受控骨干随机3-SAT基准测试上，混合方法的中位冲突减少80.8-85.5%，中位传播减少80.2-84.6%，但收益呈分布敏感性。此外，探索了轻量级机器学习门控机制来预测混合求解的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CDCL求解器在处理可满足实例时仍需大量冲突分析和布尔传播才能找到有效的搜索空间区域，求解效率有待提升。本文旨在探索是否可以通过随机低违规采样（p-bit Ising采样器）引导CDCL搜索方向，减少内部求解开销，同时保持CDCL回退机制以确保正确性。

Method: 提出混合SAT求解框架：p-bit Ising采样器生成高一致性文字，作为临时假设传递给CDCL求解器；CDCL在假设下搜索，若失败则回退至纯CDCL。在受控骨干随机3-SAT基准上评估，并训练随机森林门控模型预测混合求解的收益。

Result: 相比纯CDCL，混合方法中位冲突减少80.8-85.5%，中位传播减少80.2-84.6%。收益呈分布敏感性，仅对特定实例类有效。随机森林门控模型保留了94.8%的混合求解获胜实例。

Conclusion: p-bit引导的混合SAT求解可显著减少CDCL内部搜索开销，但收益依赖于实例分布；轻量级门控机制能有效识别适合混合求解的实例，避免无效调用。

Abstract: Boolean satisfiability (SAT) solvers are widely used in hardware verification, cryptanalysis, automatic test-pattern generation, and side-channel reasoning workflows. Modern conflict-driven clause-learning (CDCL) solvers are highly effective, but satisfiable instances may still require substantial conflict analysis and Boolean propagation before identifying productive regions of the search space. This paper studies a hybrid SAT-solving framework in which a probabilistic-bit (p-bit) Ising sampler proposes high-agreement literals that are passed to CDCL as temporary assumptions. The goal is not to replace CDCL, but to evaluate whether stochastic low-violation samples can reduce CDCL internal search effort while retaining correctness through CDCL fallback. On selected controlled-backbone random 3-SAT benchmarks, the hybrid method reduces median conflicts by 80.8-85.5% and median propagations by 80.2-84.6% relative to pure CDCL. The observed benefit is distribution-sensitive, suggesting that p-bit guidance is effective only for certain instance classes. We further report exploratory machine-learning gates that estimate when hybrid solving is likely to help. On the selected run, a random-forest gate retains 94.8% of hybrid wins, indicating that lightweight gating may help avoid unproductive hybrid calls.

</details>


### [124] [Distributed Deep Variational Approach for Privacy-preserving Data Release](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03069)
*Zahir Alsulaimawi,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出高斯隐私保护器(GPP)，通过变分互信息下界训练随机编码器压缩原始数据为低维净化表示，实现敏感属性保护与效用属性保留的权衡，并扩展至联邦学习场景，在三个基准上达到接近无约束基线的效用同时将攻击者AUC降至随机猜测水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中梯度、模型更新和输出表示仍可能泄露敏感属性，单纯的"数据不离本地"保护不完整，需要更严格的实例级隐私保护机制。

Method: GPP学习一个随机编码器，将连续高维输入映射到低维净化表示；编码器通过变分互信息下界最小化释放表示与敏感属性间的互信息，同时用交叉熵项保留效用属性，通过拉格朗日乘子β控制权衡；扩展至联邦设置后，客户端训练本地编码器，敏感标签不离客户端，聚合器仅接收净化表示。

Result: 在MNIST(数字和vs奇偶)、CelebA(微笑vs性别)、HAPT-Recognition(活动vs主体身份)三个基准上，GPP效用接近无约束自编码器基线(差距约1个百分点)，同时将攻击者AUC降至接近随机猜测。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保护敏感属性隐私的同时有效保留了任务效用，且能无缝集成到联邦学习框架中提供实例级隐私保护。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) lets distributed nodes train a shared model without exchanging their raw data, but in privacy-sensitive deployments medical sensors, IoT devices, wearables the protection offered by keeping data local is incomplete: gradients, model updates, and the released representations themselves can leak sensitive attributes. We propose the \emph{Gaussian Privacy Protector} (GPP), a data-release framework for continuous, high-dimensional inputs that learns a stochastic encoder mapping raw data to a low-dimensional sanitized representation. The encoder is trained against a variational lower bound on the mutual information between the released representation and a designated sensitive attribute, while a separate cross-entropy term preserves a designated utility attribute, with a Lagrange multiplier $β$ controlling the trade-off. We then extend GPP to the federated setting, in which each client trains a local encoder, sensitive labels never leave the client, and the aggregator receives only sanitized representations giving instance-level privacy protection in addition to the standard ``raw data stays local'' guarantee of FL. We evaluate GPP on MNIST (digit-sum utility, parity sensitive), CelebA (smiling vs.\ gender), and HAPT-Recognition (activity vs.\ subject identity). Across all three benchmarks, GPP attains utility within roughly one percentage point of an unconstrained autoencoder baseline while reducing the adversary's AUC to near random guessing.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Dynamic Vine Copulas: Detecting and Quantifying Time-Varying Higher-Order Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03061)
*Houman Safaai,Alessandro Marin Vargas*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出动态藤蔓连接函数（DVC）框架，用于估计和诊断时间序列中的非高斯依赖性变化。DVC通过固定藤蔓分解、平滑参数轨迹或正则化族切换路径刻画时变依赖，并利用全藤蔓与1-截断藤蔓的评分对比作为诊断指标，区分成对依赖与条件依赖。实验表明DVC能检测到高斯动态模型遗漏的尾部自由度变化、Clayton-to-Gumbel切换及条件交互，并在神经科学数据上识别出可复现的跨脑区同步信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态相关模型（如DCC、动态高斯图模型）主要捕捉相关系数的时变，但许多多元系统的依赖变化体现在尾部行为、非对称性或条件依赖结构上，而相关系数变化很小。因此需要一种能灵活捕捉非高斯时变依赖且具有可解释诊断能力的框架。

Method: 提出DVC框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 固定藤蔓分解以保持时间可比性，支持C-/D-/R-vines；(2) 对pair copula的时变建模采用平滑参数轨迹或正则化族切换路径；(3) 核心诊断指标为全藤蔓与1-截断藤蔓的对数评分之差，理论上对应藤蔓总相关分解的高阶树项，实践中作为预测诊断量。

Result: 合成数据实验表明DVC能检测到Student-t尾部自由度变化、Clayton-to-Gumbel族切换以及递归条件交互，而高斯动态基线方法无法识别或混淆这些模式。在Allen Visual Behavior Neuropixels神经科学数据上，DVC识别出可复现的时变高阶树信号，该信号在留出分割中保持正值，在去相关零模型下消失，指示跨脑区同步依赖。

Conclusion: DVC既是一个灵活的时变连接函数模型，也是一个可解释的诊断工具，能够区分时变依赖变化究竟是成对层面的还是条件层面的。其诊断指标在成对依赖场景下接近零，在条件交互场景下显著上升，具有良好的选择性和可解释性。

Abstract: Time varying dependence is often modeled through dynamic correlations or Gaussian graphical models, yet many multivariate systems change through tail behavior, asymmetry, or conditional structure while correlations change little. We introduce Dynamic Vine Copulas (DVC), a temporal vine copula framework for estimating and diagnosing sequence wide non-Gaussian dependence. DVC keeps a chosen vine factorization fixed for comparability, can use C-, D-, or R-vines, and couples pair copula states across time through smooth parameter trajectories or temporally regularized family switching paths. Its central diagnostic contrasts held-out scores from a full vine and its matched 1-truncated counterpart, separating flexible first-tree pairwise evidence from higher-tree conditional evidence. At the population level, under a correct fixed vine and simplifying assumption, this contrast is the higher-tree term of a vine total correlation decomposition; in finite samples, it is a predictive diagnostic. Across controlled benchmarks, DVC detects Student-t tail degree changes, Clayton-to-Gumbel switches, and recurrent conditional interaction episodes that Gaussian dynamic baselines miss or conflate. The higher-tree score stays near zero in pairwise only regimes but rises selectively during conditional interaction regimes. On Allen Visual Behavior Neuropixels data, DVC identifies a reproducible time indexed higher-tree signal that is positive across held-out splits and disappears under a decorrelated null, indicating simultaneous cross-area dependence. Together, these results show that DVC is both a flexible temporal copula model and an interpretable diagnostic for whether time varying dependence changes are pairwise or conditional.

</details>


### [126] [Intrinsic effective sample size for manifold-valued Markov chain Monte Carlo via kernel discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03266)
*Kisung You*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于核差异的内在有效样本量（ESS），用于马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）在流形值空间上的输出评估。该指标具有坐标无关性、有限样本风险解释和渐近积分自相关表示，且当核尊重状态空间几何时，可提供无诊断偏差的诊断工具。实验证明了旋转不变性及与经验分布误差的校准性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有效样本量（ESS）针对标量或欧几里得空间定义，在流形值样本（如旋转、图表变换或不同嵌入）下不是规范的选择——坐标方向的ESS会因旋转或坐标变换而发生改变。因此需要一种内在的、与坐标选择无关的有效样本量诊断方法。

Method: 利用核差异（kernel discrepancy）构造内在有效样本量：定义为使经验分布与目标分布之间的期望平方核差异相等的独立样本数量。具体包括：（1）建立有限样本风险解释和渐近积分自相关表示；（2）证明在传输核下的不变性、算子与主方向解释，以及滞后窗口估计量在绝对正则性条件下的相合性；（3）讨论流形上有效核的构造，指出测地线高斯核在弯曲空间上一般不具正定性。

Result: ①提出了一种坐标无关的内在有效样本量，并给出其严格数学定义和理论性质（不变性、算子解释、估计量相合性）；②指出测地线高斯核在弯曲流形上非正定的重要发现；③通过球面实验验证了旋转不变性以及与经验分布误差的校准效果。

Conclusion: 本文为流形值MCMC输出提供了一种内在的、几何感知的有效样本量诊断工具，克服了传统ESS对坐标选择的依赖，并建立了完整的理论和估计框架。

Abstract: Effective sample size is a standard summary of Markov chain Monte Carlo output, but it is usually attached to scalar or Euclidean summaries chosen by the analyst. For manifold-valued samples this choice is not canonical: coordinate-wise effective sample sizes can change under rotations, chart changes, or alternative embeddings of the same underlying path. We propose an intrinsic effective sample size based on kernel discrepancy. The proposed quantity is the number of independent draws that would yield the same expected squared kernel discrepancy between the empirical distribution and the target distribution. This gives an exact finite-sample risk interpretation, an asymptotic integrated-autocorrelation representation, and a coordinate-free diagnostic whenever the kernel respects the geometry of the state space. We establish invariance under transported kernels, operator and principal-direction interpretations, and consistency of a lag-window estimator under boundedness and absolute-regularity conditions. We also discuss valid kernel constructions on manifolds, emphasizing that geodesic Gaussian kernels are not generally positive definite on curved spaces. Sphere experiments illustrate rotation invariance and calibration of the proposed diagnostic against empirical distributional error.

</details>


### [127] [Partial Effective Information Decomposition for Synergistic Causality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03267)
*Mingzhe Yang,Shuo Wang,Jiang Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出部分有效信息分解（PEID）框架，用于在多变量因果关系中分解唯一因果信息和协同因果信息，为复杂系统中的协同因果关系提供了统一的干预主义信息论工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果分析方法在处理复杂系统中的协同因果关系时存在不足，缺乏基于干预主义因果关系的多变量分解框架。

Method: 提出部分有效信息分解（PEID）框架，在最大熵干预下将多个源变量对目标变量的影响分解为唯一信息和协同信息，并基于此定义包含超边的因果图及向下因果关系。

Result: 在三变量情形下，PEID与部分信息分解（PID）的主要公理兼容；在最大熵干预下，输入变量间的相关性被消除，冗余消失，从而可计算协同关系；在KnowAir-V2空气质量预测任务中，PEID能从学习到的动力学模型中提取可解释的站间因果结构。

Conclusion: PEID为分析复杂系统中的多变量及协同因果机制提供了一般的干预主义信息论工具。

Abstract: Causality is a central topic in scientific inquiry, yet for complex systems, the identification and analysis of synergistic causation remain a challenging and fundamental problem. In the context of causal relations among multivariate variables, a decomposition framework grounded in interventionist causation is still lacking. To address this gap, this paper proposes Partial Effective Information Decomposition (PEID), a framework that decomposes the influence of multiple source variables on a target variable under maximum-entropy interventions into unique and synergistic information, thereby providing a unified and computable characterization of synergistic causal relations. Theoretically, in the three-variable case, the proposed framework is compatible with the major axioms of Partial Information Decomposition (PID). Empirically, under maximum-entropy interventions, correlations among input variables are removed, causing redundancy to vanish and thereby enabling PEID to compute synergistic relations. Furthermore, based on this framework, it is possible to define causal graphs containing hyperedges as well as downward causation, thus offering a unified toolkit for analyzing cross-scale and multivariate causal mechanisms in complex systems. Finally, applying the framework to a machine-learning-based air quality forecasting task on KnowAir-V2, we demonstrate that PEID can extract interpretable inter-station causal structures from a learned dynamical model. These results suggest that PEID provides a general interventionist information-theoretic tool for analyzing multivariate and synergistic causal mechanisms in complex systems.

</details>


### [128] [On the Spectral Structure and Objective Equivalence of Orthogonal Multilabel Fisher Discriminants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03283)
*Brian Keith-Norambuena,Juan Bekios-Calfa*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对多标签线性判别分析（LDA）进行了统一的理论分析，涵盖代数结构和统计保证两方面。代数上，刻画了多标签类间散度矩阵的秩，证明有效判别维度可突破单标签C-1上限；建立了多标签方差分解，证明了四种Fisher目标在特定约束下的等价性，并给出标签距离保持界。统计上，建立了子空间估计误差的有限样本上界（O(k_max √(d log d/n)/gap_r)）和匹配的极小化最优下界（Ω(σ² d/(n·gap_r))），实现了近最优率。还提供了距离集中性、鲁棒性和正则化分析。数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LDA理论主要针对单标签分类，缺乏对多标签场景下代数结构和统计保-证的统一理论分析，特别是多标签LDA的判别维度是否突破C-1限制、Fisher目标等价性条件、以及子空间估计的统计速率等问题尚未解决。

Method: 采用多标签散度矩阵公式与Stiefel正交约束相结合的理论框架，从代数角度推导散度矩阵秩、方差分解、Fisher目标等价性及距离保持界；从统计角度基于次高斯噪声假设，推导子空间估计误差的上下界，并分析距离集中性、鲁棒性和正则化性质。

Result: 1) 有效判别维度可严格超过C-1；2) 在W^T S_t^ML W = I_r约束下四种Fisher目标等价，在Stiefel约束下出现分歧；3) 投影距离与Hamming距离满足双边保持界；4) 子空间估计误差上界为O(k_max √(d log d/n)/gap_r)，下界为Ω(σ² d/(n·gap_r))，近极小化最优。

Conclusion: 该工作为多标签LDA建立了统一的代数-统计理论框架，揭示了多标签特有量（k_max, κ(S_t^ML), ‖Γ/n‖₂, Δ_r）对统计界的主导作用，为后续面向应用的多标签判别分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We provide a unified theoretical analysis of Linear Discriminant Analysis with simultaneous multilabel scatter matrix formulations and Stiefel orthogonality constraints. Our contributions span both algebraic structure and statistical guarantees. On the algebraic side, we characterize the rank of the multilabel between-class scatter matrix, showing that the effective discriminant dimensionality can strictly exceed the classical single-label bound of $C-1$; we establish a multilabel partition of variance and prove that all four Fisher objectives are equivalent under the $W^\top S_t^{ML} W = I_r$ constraint while characterizing their divergence under the Stiefel constraint; and we prove a two-sided label-distance preservation bound relating projected distances to Hamming distances in label space. On the statistical side, we establish a finite-sample $O(k_{\max}\sqrt{d\log d/n}/gap_r)$ bound on the subspace estimation error under sub-Gaussian noise with a matching $Ω(σ^2 d/(n\,gap_r))$ minimax lower bound, establishing a near-minimax-optimal rate (matching up to logarithmic and $k_{\max}$ factors) for multilabel discriminant subspace estimation. We further provide high-probability distance concentration, robustness guarantees under label interactions, and a regularization analysis preserving the spectral structure when $d \gg n$. All results are verified numerically on synthetic data generated from the linear label-effect model, covering both the algebraic identities and the multilabel-specific quantities ($k_{\max}$, $κ(S_t^{ML})$, $\|Γ/n\|_2$, $Δ_r$) that govern the statistical bounds. The numerical experiments are designed as a sanity check for the theorems rather than as an empirical benchmark; evaluation on real multilabel datasets is left to future work targeting application-oriented venues.

</details>


### [129] [Adaptive Estimation and Optimal Control in Offline Contextual MDPs without Stationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03393)
*Riddhiman Bhattacharyya,Sayak Chakrabarty,Imon Banerjee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对上下文马尔可夫决策过程（Contextual MDPs）的自适应估计与成本优化新方法，该估计器具有强最优性保证，通过T估计技术克服了非平稳性和模型不规则性等关键挑战，并在两种损失函数下推导了oracle风险界，同时为成本函数提供了有限样本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文MDP在生物统计和机器学习等领域有广泛应用，但将其适配到离线数据集一直缺乏鲁棒且理论上有依据的方法。

Method: 利用T估计统计技术，提出了首个具有强最优性保证的自适应估计器；针对上下文MDP的内生属性（非平稳性、模型不规则性）设计了估计量选择程序，并在两种损失函数下推导oracle风险界，进而利用密度估计确定最优控制并给出成本函数的有限样本保证。

Result: 在完全一般性下建立了强最优性保证；推导了两种不同损失函数下的oracle风险界；为成本函数提供了有限样本保证。

Conclusion: 该工作为离线上下文MDP的估计与优化提供了首个理论上健全的自适应方法，填补了该领域缺乏鲁棒理论方法的空白。

Abstract: Contextual MDPs are powerful tools with wide applicability in areas from biostatistics to machine learning. However, specializing them to offline datasets has been challenging due to a lack of robust, theoretically backed methods. Our work tackles this problem by introducing a new approach towards adaptive estimation and cost optimization of contextual MDPs. This estimator, to the best of our knowledge, is the first of its kind, and is endowed with strong optimality guarantees. We achieve this by overcoming the key technical challenges evolving from the endogenous properties of contextual MDPs; such as non-stationarity, or model irregularity. Our guarantees are established under complete generality by utilizing the relatively recent and powerful statistical technique of $T$-estimation (Baraud, 2011). We first provide a procedure for selecting an estimator given a sample from a contextual MDP and use it to derive oracle risk bounds under two distinct, but nevertheless meaningful, loss functions. We then consider the problem of determining the optimal control with the aid of the aforementioned density estimate and provide finite sample guarantees for the cost function.

</details>


### [130] [Stochastic Schrödinger Diffusion Models for Pure-State Ensemble Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03573)
*Jian Xu,Wei Chen. Chao Li,Jingyuan Zheng,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出随机薛定谔扩散模型(SSDMs)，在复射影空间上构建基于得分的生成框架，通过局部欧几里得近似实现量子纯态系综的生成，并在下游QML任务中提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习中，经典数据常编码为量子纯态，需要在表示层面进行生成建模。然而，现有的基于得分的扩散模型难以直接应用于复射影空间这一非欧几何流形，因为转移密度不可解析计算，且反向时间采样器难以定义。

Method: 在复射影空间上赋予Fubini-Study度规，利用随机薛定谔方程实现正向黎曼扩散；推导由黎曼得分驱动的反向时间动力学；为绕过转移密度不可解析问题，提出基于局部欧几里得Ornstein-Uhlenbeck近似的局部时间目标函数，在FS法坐标中计算教师得分并映射回流形。

Result: 实验表明，SSDMs能够忠实捕获目标纯态系综的统计特性，包括可观测量矩、重叠核MMD和纠缠度量；生成的量子表示通过表示级数据增强提升了下游QML的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SSDMs成功将基于得分的扩散模型推广到量子纯态流形，解决了非欧几何下的生成建模难题，并为QML提供了有效的表示级数据增强工具。

Abstract: In quantum machine learning (QML), classical data are often encoded as quantum pure states and processed directly as quantum representations, motivating representation-level generative modeling that samples new quantum states from an underlying pure-state ensemble rather than re-preparing them from perturbed classical inputs. However, extending \emph{score-based} diffusion models with well-defined reverse-time samplers to quantum pure-state ensembles remains challenging, due to the non-Euclidean geometry of the complex projective space $\mathbb{CP}^{d-1}$ and the intractability of transition densities. We propose \emph{Stochastic Schrödinger Diffusion Models} (SSDMs), an intrinsic score-based generative framework on $\mathbb{CP}^{d-1}$ endowed with the Fubini--Study (FS) metric. SSDMs formulate a forward Riemannian diffusion with a stochastic Schrödinger equation (SSE) realization, and derive reverse-time dynamics driven by the Riemannian score $\nabla_{\mathrm{FS}} \log p_t$. To enable training without analytic transition densities, we introduce a local-time objective based on a local Euclidean Ornstein--Uhlenbeck approximation in FS normal coordinates, yielding an analytic teacher score mapped back to the manifold. Experiments show that SSDMs faithfully capture target pure-state ensemble statistics, including observable moments, overlap-kernel MMD, and entanglement measures, and that SSDM-generated quantum representations improve downstream QML generalization via representation-level data augmentation.

</details>


### [131] [Amortized Variational Inference for Joint Posterior and Predictive Distributions in Bayesian Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03710)
*Nan Feng,Xun Huan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种变分贝叶斯框架，直接目标后验预测分布，联合学习后验和预测分布的变分近似，通过变分上界和矩正则化项实现端到端训练，显著降低在线预测推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯预测推理采用两阶段流程（先近似后验，再通过蒙特卡洛传播不确定性），对于高保真模型（如PDE控制模型）计算成本高昂，亟需更高效的方法。

Method: 构建直接针对后验预测分布的变分贝叶斯框架；引入KL散度的变分上界及基于矩的正则化项；采用摊销训练方式将计算转移到离线阶段。

Result: 在分析基准测试和有限元固体力学问题中，该方法比传统两阶段变分推理获得了更准确的预测分布，同时大幅降低了在线推理成本。

Conclusion: 所提出的端到端变分预测推理框架在预测精度和计算效率上均优于传统两阶段方法，尤其适用于高保真模型场景。

Abstract: Bayesian predictive inference propagates parameter uncertainty to quantities of interest through the posterior-predictive distribution. In practice, this is typically performed using a two-stage procedure: first approximating the posterior distribution of model parameters, and then propagating posterior samples through the predictive model via Monte Carlo simulation. This sequential workflow can be computationally demanding, particularly for high-fidelity models such as those governed by partial differential equations.
  We propose a variational Bayesian framework that directly targets the posterior-predictive distribution and jointly learns variational approximations of both the posterior and the corresponding predictive distribution. The formulation introduces a variational upper bound on the Kullback--Leibler divergence together with moment-based regularization terms. The variational distributions are trained in an amortized manner, shifting computational effort to an offline stage and enabling efficient online inference. Numerical experiments ranging from analytical benchmarks to a finite-element solid mechanics problem demonstrate that the proposed method achieves more accurate predictive distributions than conventional two-stage variational inference, while substantially reducing the cost of online predictive inference.

</details>


### [132] [Tempered Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03712)
*Andreas Makris,Paul Fearnhead,Chris Nemeth*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TGD是一种基于退火序列蒙特卡洛的无训练条件扩散采样框架，通过粒子重加权、重采样和传播机制，在保持后验近似一致性的同时，提升计算效率。A-TGD进一步在采样中后期剪枝为单轨迹，在图像逆问题中实现更优的速度-质量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练条件扩散采样器计算效率低下：独立引导轨迹质量差异大，且单轨迹上增加函数评估次数无法弥补早期不良决策。

Method: 提出TGD框架，针对干净信号上的退火后验分布，仅将含噪扩散状态作为辅助变量用于提出重建和传播粒子。粒子通过增量似然比重加权、重采样并在不同噪声水平间传播。A-TGD在采样中后期剪枝为单条高似然轨迹。

Result: 在二维逆问题和图像逆问题实验中，TGD改进了后验近似，A-TGD在壁钟时间与采样质量上优于独立多轨迹基线方法。

Conclusion: TGD框架通过粒子滤波思想集中计算资源于先验与观测一致的轨迹，在理想精确重建假设下，随粒子数增长可一致近似后验分布。

Abstract: Training-free conditional diffusion provides a flexible alternative to task-specific conditional model training, but existing samplers often allocate computation inefficiently: independent guided trajectories can vary widely in quality, and additional function evaluations along a single trajectory may not recover from poor early decisions. We propose Tempered Guided Diffusion (TGD), an annealed sequential Monte Carlo framework for training-free conditional sampling with diffusion priors. TGD targets tempered posterior distributions over the clean signal, using noisy diffusion states only as auxiliary variables for proposing reconstructions and propagating particles. Particles are reweighted by incremental likelihood ratios, resampled, and propagated across noise levels, concentrating computation on trajectories plausible under both the prior and observation. Under idealized exact-reconstruction assumptions, full TGD yields a consistent particle approximation to the posterior as the number of particles grows. For expensive reconstruction tasks, Accelerated TGD (A-TGD) retains early particle exploration but prunes to a single high-likelihood trajectory partway through sampling. Experiments on a controlled two-dimensional inverse problem and image inverse problems show improved posterior approximation and favorable wall-clock speed-quality tradeoffs over independent multi-trajectory baselines.

</details>


### [133] [Predicting missing values: A good idea?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03733)
*Stef van Buuren*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文论证了最小化MSE的预测性插补会系统性降低数据自然变异性，导致下游分析偏差；而通过添加与MSE成比例的噪声，随机插补能有效消除这些偏差，并指出MSE不是衡量插补质量的合适指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中广泛使用最小化MSE进行缺失值插补，但这种做法在下游分析中引入系统性偏差（如方差、相关性、斜率等参数估计有偏），根本原因在于MSE最优的插补值是平均值，降低了数据自然变异性。

Method: 通过多元正态分布的模拟案例，对比预测性插补（最小化MSE）与随机插补（添加与MSE成比例的噪声）的效果；并评估missForest、softImpute和mice三种流行插补工具。

Result: 预测性插补方法系统性引入偏差，而随机插补保留数据自然变异性并产生无偏估计；三种流行插补工具中预测性方法均表现出一致偏差。

Conclusion: MSE不足以衡量插补质量，因其优先保证点估计准确性而牺牲变异性；在插补中引入噪声对于防止偏差和确保有效下游分析至关重要，应重视随机插补方法处理不完整数据。

Abstract: Minimizing the Mean Squared Error (MSE) is a key objective in machine learning and is commonly used for imputing missing values. While this approach provides accurate point estimates, it introduces systematic biases in downstream analyses. These biases affect key parameters such as variance, prevalence, correlation, slope, and explained variance. The root cause is that imputed values optimized for MSE are averages, which reduce the natural variability in the data.
  This paper demonstrates that adding noise to imputed values can effectively eliminate these biases. The required noise level is proportional to the MSE. Using a toy example in a multivariate normal setting, we compare two methods: predictive imputation, which minimizes MSE, and stochastic imputation, which incorporates random noise. Simulation results show that predictive methods systematically introduce bias, while stochastic methods preserve the data's natural variability and produce unbiased estimates.
  We also evaluate three popular imputation tools -- missForest, softImpute, and mice -- and observe consistent biases in predictive methods. These findings highlight that MSE is an inadequate measure of imputation quality, as it prioritizes accuracy over variability. Incorporating noise into imputation methods is essential to prevent biases and ensure valid downstream analyses, underscoring the importance of stochastic approaches for handling incomplete data.

</details>


### [134] [Low Rank Tensor Completion via Adaptive ADMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03736)
*Niclas Führling,Getuar Rexhepi,Giuseppe Thadeu Freitas de Abreu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于ADMM框架的低秩张量补全算法，通过交替求解子问题和闭式近端算子迭代优化，结合过松弛与自适应惩罚参数更新，在NMSE指标上超越现有主流方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对部分观测低秩张量的补全问题，现有基于核范数最小化的方法存在收敛慢、性能有限等不足，亟需更高效的求解算法。

Method: 基于ADMM优化框架，将核范数最小化问题分解为多个子问题，利用闭式近端算子迭代求解；引入过松弛技术和自适应惩罚参数更新策略加速收敛、提升性能。

Result: 在归一化均方误差(NMSE)指标上，该方法显著优于传统核范数最小化方法及核范数与矩阵分解的混合方法；通过将SotA的解作为初始化可进一步提升收敛速度。

Conclusion: 提出的ADMM-based低秩张量补全方法在精度和收敛性上均优于现有SotA技术，是一种高效、鲁棒的张量补全方案。

Abstract: We consider a novel algorithm, for the completion of partially observed low-rank tensors, as a generalization of matrix completion. The proposed low-rank tensor completion (TC) method builds on the conventional nuclear norm (NN) minimization-based low-rank TC paradigm, by leveraging the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) optimization framework. To that extend the original NN minimization problem is reformulated into multiple subproblems, which are then solved iteratively via closed-form proximal operators, making use of over-relaxation and an adaptive penalty parameter update scheme, to further speed up convergence and improve the overall performance of the method. Simulation results demonstrate the superior performance of the new method in terms of normalized mean square error (NMSE), compared to the conventional state-of-the-art (SotA) techniques, including NN minimization approaches, as well as a mixture of the latter with a matrix factorization approach, while its convergence can be significantly improved by initializing the algorithm with the solution of the SotA.

</details>


### [135] [Training-Free Probabilistic Time-Series Forecasting with Conformal Seasonal Pools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03789)
*Valery Manokhin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Conformal Seasonal Pools (CSP)，一种无需训练的概率时间序列预测方法，通过在季节性朴素预测周围混合同季节经验样本与带符号残差样本实现预测。在六个数据集的滚动基准测试中，CSP-Adaptive在CRPS、归一化分位数损失和95%经验覆盖率上显著优于DeepNPTS，速度提升超500倍。CSP无参数、无训练，论文主张无训练保形采样器应作为学习型非参数预测器的强制基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习概率预测方法（如DeepNPTS）虽然灵活，但存在校准失败的问题：其名义95%预测区间实际覆盖率仅约66%，且在10%最差窗口内整个多步预测轨迹完全偏离真实值，这对安全关键型应用（医疗、金融、能源、自动驾驶）构成严重风险。此外，这些方法训练成本高、计算开销大。

Method: CSP是一种无训练的概率预测方法：以季节性朴素预测为中心，混合同季节的经验样本（historical seasonal draws）与带符号残差样本（signed residual draws），利用保形推断（conformal inference）原理进行自适应调整，无需学习任何参数。

Result: 在电力、汇率、太阳能、出租车、流量、维基百科六个数据集上，CSP-Adaptive在所有指标上显著优于DeepNPTS：CRPS（Wilcoxon p≈4×10^{-10}）、归一化分位数损失（p≈7×10^{-10}）、95%经验覆盖率（p≈8×10^{-45}，均值0.89 vs 0.66），且CPU运行速度快500倍以上。

Conclusion: 训练-free的保形采样器（如CSP）应作为评估学习型非参数概率预测器的强制基线。DeepNPTS在安全关键场景中存在严重校准失败问题，而CSP在无训练、无参数的情况下实现了更好的覆盖率和计算效率。

Abstract: We propose Conformal Seasonal Pools (CSP), a training-free probabilistic time-series forecaster that mixes same-season empirical draws with signed residual draws around a seasonal naive forecast. In an audited rolling-origin benchmark on the six time-series datasets where DeepNPTS was originally evaluated (electricity, exchange_rate, solar_energy, taxi, traffic, wikipedia), CSP-Adaptive significantly outperforms DeepNPTS on every metric we report -- CRPS (per-window paired Wilcoxon $p \approx 4 \times 10^{-10}$), normalized mean quantile loss ($p \approx 7 \times 10^{-10}$), and empirical 95% coverage ($p \approx 8 \times 10^{-45}$, mean 0.89 vs 0.66) -- while running over 500x faster on CPU. Coverage is the most decision-critical of these: a 0.95 nominal interval that contains the truth in only ~66% of cases fails the basic calibration desideratum and would not survive deployment in safety- or decision-critical settings. The failure mode is also more severe than aggregate coverage suggests: in the worst 10% of windows, DeepNPTS's prediction interval covers none of the H forecast horizons -- the entire multi-step trajectory misses the truth at every step simultaneously. This poses serious risk in safety- and decision-critical applications such as healthcare, finance, energy operations, and autonomous systems, where prediction intervals that systematically miss the truth across the entire planning horizon translate directly into misclassified patients, regulatory capital failures, grid imbalances, and safety-case violations. CSP achieves all of this with no learned parameters and no training. We argue training-free conformal samplers should be mandatory baselines when evaluating learned non-parametric forecasters.

</details>


### [136] [The Manokhin Probability Matrix: A Diagnostic Framework for Classifier Probability Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03816)
*Valery Manokhin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Manokhin概率矩阵，一个将Brier评分分解为可靠性（校准误差）和分辨率（判别能力）的二维诊断框架，通过2×2网格将分类器分为四类原型（Eagle, Bull, Sloth, Mole），并基于大规模实证研究证明：判别能力难以通过事后校准提升，而校准是可修复的，因此应优先优化判别能力，再事后修复校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brier评分将预测概率的两个独立属性——可靠性（校准误差）和分辨率（判别能力）——混为一谈，导致研究者无法区分分类器的具体缺陷，也难以制定针对性的优化策略。

Method: 引入Manokhin概率矩阵，以Spiegelhalter Z统计量（校准误差）和AUC-ROC期望秩（判别能力）为坐标轴，构建2×2诊断网格，将分类器分为四类原型。在21个分类器、5种事后校准方法、30个真实二分类任务上开展大规模实证研究。

Result: CatBoost等为Eagle（两项均优）；XGBoost等为Bull（判别强、校准差），Venn-Abers校准可降低其6.5-12.6%的log-loss但会损害Eagle 2.1%；SVM等为Sloth（校准好、判别弱）；MLP等为Mole（两项均差）。理论证明（命题1）：任何保序的事后校准器都无法增加判别能力。

Conclusion: 校准是可修复的，判别能力是难以提升的，实践中应优先优化判别能力，再事后修复校准，而不应直接优化聚合的Brier评分。

Abstract: The Brier score conflates two distinct properties of probabilistic predictions: reliability (calibration error) and resolution (discriminatory power). We introduce the Manokhin Probability Matrix, a BCG-style two-dimensional diagnostic framework that separates them. Classifiers are placed on a 2x2 grid by Spiegelhalter Z-statistic and AUC-ROC expected rank, then assigned to one of four archetypes: Eagle (good on both axes), Bull (strong discrimination, poor calibration), Sloth (well-calibrated, weak discriminator), and Mole (poor on both). Each archetype carries a distinct prescription. We populate the matrix from a large-scale empirical study spanning 21 classifiers, 5 post-hoc calibrators, and 30 real-world binary classification tasks from the TabArena-v0.1 suite. The assignment is unambiguous. CatBoost, TabICL, EBM, TabPFN, GBC, and Random Forest are Eagles. XGBoost, LightGBM, and HGB are Bulls; Venn-Abers calibration cuts log-loss by 6.5 to 12.6% on Bulls but degrades Eagles by 2.1%. SVM, LR, LDA, and the empirical base-rate predictor are Sloths. MLP, KNN, Naive Bayes, and ExtraTrees are Moles. A theoretical asymmetry follows: no order-preserving post-hoc calibrator can add discriminatory power (Proposition 1), so calibration is the fixable part and discrimination is the hard part. The practical rule is direct: do not optimise aggregate Brier score without first decomposing it; optimise discrimination first, then fix calibration post-hoc. Code and raw experimental data are available at https://github.com/valeman/classifier_calibration.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [Causal Small Area Estimation with Survey-only Covariates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03285)
*Tsubasa Ito,Shonosuke Sugasawa*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对仅有样本单元可观测处理变量和结果变量、而辅助协变量可获取全体总体的现实数据结构，提出了一种新的区域特异性处理效应识别策略，并开发了双重稳健估计量，该估计量在结果回归模型或处理与区域分配模型中任一正确设定时均保持一致，且在正则条件下达到半参数有效界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果小区域估计方法通常假设处理状态在总体所有单元中均可观测，这一假设在调查场景中往往不成立——实际中处理变量和结果变量仅对样本单元观测，而辅助协变量可获取总体信息。为填补这一方法空白，需要发展更贴合实际数据结构的识别与估计策略。

Method: 提出一种新的识别策略，结合仅调查可得的协变量与总体层面的辅助信息；基于此构建双重稳健估计量，该估计量在结果回归模型或处理与区域分配模型中任一正确设定时均一致；推导了目标参数的半参数有效界；通过模拟研究评估有限样本表现，并进行了实证应用。

Result: 模拟研究表明该方法在区域样本量较小的场景下具有优越的有限样本表现；提出的估计量在正则条件下达到了半参数有效界。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了实际调查中处理变量和结果变量仅可观测于样本单元的限制，为小区域因果推断提供了更实用的方法论支持。

Abstract: Area-specific causal inference is important in many policy and survey applications, where the goal is to evaluate treatment effects for small geographic or demographic domains. Existing causal small area estimation methods, however, typically rely on a strong data requirement that treatment status is observed for all units in the population. This assumption is often unrealistic in practical survey settings, where both treatment and outcome variables are observed only for sampled units, while auxiliary covariates are available for the full population. To address this limitation, we develop a new identification strategy for area-specific treatment effects under this more realistic data structure by combining survey-only covariates with population-level auxiliary information. Based on this result, we propose a doubly robust estimator that remains consistent when either the outcome regression model or the treatment and area assignment models are correctly specified. We further derive the semiparametric efficiency bound for the target parameter and show that the proposed estimator attains this bound under regularity conditions. Simulation studies demonstrate favorable finite-sample performance, particularly in settings with small sample sizes within areas, and an empirical application illustrates the practical relevance of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [138] [Smoothed estimation of Wasserstein barycenters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03300)
*Pengtao Li,Changbo Zhu,Xiaohui Chen*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究精确Wasserstein重心（barycenter）的统计估计问题。针对经验重心存在的严重维度灾难，提出一种结合密度估计与Sobolev几何结构的平滑感知方法，利用半对偶公式和Sobolev优化几何，建立了估计重心泛函及其最小化者的非参数收敛速率，证明平滑性可显著提升统计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经验Wasserstein重心在非渐近结果中存在严重的维度灾难问题，即统计误差随维度指数级增长，限制了高维应用。作者希望利用重心问题的半对偶形式及Sobolev优化几何结构，开发一种对平滑性敏感的方法来克服这一困难。

Method: 基于Wasserstein重心问题的半对偶（semi-dual）公式，利用其对应的Sobolev优化几何结构，提出将密度估计与Sobolev几何结构相结合的平滑感知方法。通过这种方式对总体重心进行估计，而非直接使用经验重心。

Result: 建立了估计重心泛函及其最小化者的非参数收敛速率，证明了平滑性可以显著提高统计性能，有效缓解了维度灾难问题。

Conclusion: 平滑性感知方法能够有效改善Wasserstein重心估计的统计效率，为高维最优传输问题提供了一条可行的理论路径。

Abstract: This paper studies the statistical estimation of exact Wasserstein barycenters. Existing non-asymptotic results for empirical barycenters exhibit a severe curse of dimensionality. Motivated by the semi-dual formulation of the barycenter problem and its associated Sobolev optimization geometry, we develop a smoothness-aware approach that combines density estimation with Sobolev geometric structure to estimate the population barycenter. We establish nonparametric convergence rates for estimating both the barycenter functional and its minimizer, demonstrating how smoothness can substantially improve statistical performance.

</details>


### [139] [Poisson Empirical Bayes via Gamma-Smoothed Nonparametric Maximum Likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03594)
*Taehyun Kim*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对泊松均值问题中的经验贝叶斯方法，提出了一种平滑的非参数最大似然估计（smooth NPMLE），通过将先验建模为Gamma混合分布，解决了传统NPMLE的离散性和先验估计速度慢的问题，并基于此构建了经验贝叶斯置信集，实现了渐近精确的边际覆盖且区间更短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NPMLE在泊松均值问题中用于最优后验均值估计已有理论保证，但在不确定性量化（构建置信集）方面存在两大挑战：NPMLE的离散性导致置信集难以构造，以及先验估计的收敛速度慢影响置信集的渐近性质。

Method: 提出平滑NPMLE，将先验分布建模为Gamma混合分布（灵活且能逼近$(0,\infty)$上的多种连续先验），保留经典NPMLE的凸优化结构。基于此估计器，构建plug-in经验贝叶斯置信集以模仿oracle最优边际覆盖集。

Result: 平滑NPMLE达到后验均值估计的近似最优参数化收敛速度；在混合分布具有紧支撑假设下，先验和后验密度估计达到多项式收敛速度。所构建的置信集在理论上和实证上均实现渐近精确的边际覆盖，且区间长度显著短于现有方法。

Conclusion: 平滑NPMLE有效解决了传统NPMLE在不确定性量化中的离散性和慢速估计问题，为泊松均值问题提供了更优的置信集构造方案。

Abstract: Empirical Bayes methods are widely used for large-scale estimation and inference in the Poisson means problem. Existing results establish theoretical properties of the nonparametric maximum likelihood estimator (NPMLE) for optimal posterior mean estimation, but comparatively less is known about uncertainty quantification (i.e., construction of confidence sets). Two main challenges in constructing confidence sets for the latent parameters based on the NPMLE are its discreteness and its slow rate of prior estimation. We resolve these limitations by introducing a smooth NPMLE that models the prior as a Gamma mixture, which is a flexible class capable of approximating a wide range of continuous priors on $(0,\infty)$. This procedure preserves the convex optimization structure of the classical NPMLE. The smooth NPMLE achieves the optimal nearly parametric rate for posterior mean estimation. Moreover, it achieves a polynomial convergence rate for prior and posterior density estimation under a compact support assumption on the mixing distribution. Based on the smooth NPMLE, we construct plug-in empirical Bayes confidence sets that mimic the oracle optimal (in terms of expected length) marginal coverage sets. We show theoretically and empirically that these sets achieve asymptotically exact marginal coverage and are substantially shorter than existing methods.

</details>


### [140] [Statistical Inference via T-Posterior Randomised Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03674)
*Yannick Baraud*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出一种新的随机化估计方法，用于统计模型中未知分布的估计，建立了非渐近性能界，并证明了其对模型误设的鲁棒性，且无需使用集中不等式和经验过程理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统估计方法在处理模型误设时鲁棒性不足，且通常依赖集中不等式和经验过程理论来建立性能保证，本文旨在提出一种无需这些理论工具即可获得鲁棒性保证的新方法。

Method: 提出一种随机化估计方法，生成观测随机变量未知分布的随机化估计量，通过规避集中不等式和经验过程理论来建立非渐近性能界。

Result: 建立了所提估计量的非渐近性能界，证明了其对模型误设的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种不依赖传统概率不等式和经验过程理论即可获得鲁棒估计的新途径，并在泊松过程强度估计问题上得到了验证。

Abstract: Given a statistical model, we propose a novel estimation method that yields randomised estimators for the unknown distribution of an observed random variable. We establish non-asymptotic bounds for the performance of these estimators and demonstrate their robustness to potential model misspecification. Notably, these properties are established by circumventing the use of concentration inequalities and empirical process theory. We provide an illustration of this approach to the problem of estimating the intensity of a Poisson process.

</details>


### [141] [Local estimation of transition rates of jump processes through discretization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03694)
*Martin Bladt,Rasmus Frigaard Lemvig*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文从非参数角度研究了马尔可夫和半马尔可夫跳过程的泊松回归方法，允许分区中的时间和持续时间间隔长度随观测数变化。在不对真实强度做结构假设的情况下，推导了发生率/暴露率在适当分区长度收缩条件下的渐近正态性，并仅使用经典中心极限定理和计数过程基本结果证明了渐近性。通过模拟和真实数据验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化方法对跳过程强度施加了结构假设，而非参数方法在分区长度随样本量变化时的渐近性质尚未被充分研究。作者旨在填补这一理论空白，建立更灵活的建模框架。

Method: 采用非参数泊松回归方法，允许分区的时间间隔和持续时间间隔长度随观测数变化；不对真实强度函数做任何结构性假设；仅依赖经典中心极限定理和计数过程基本结果推导渐近正态性。

Result: 在分区长度适当收缩的条件下，证明了马尔可夫和半马尔可夫模型下发生率/暴露率的渐近正态性，并通过模拟和真实数据验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该非参数方法在不施加结构假设的情况下，为马尔可夫和半马尔可夫跳过程提供了渐近正态的泊松回归估计，方法理论简洁、适用性广。

Abstract: We investigate the Poisson regression method for Markov and semi-Markov jump processes from a nonparametric angle, allowing the lengths of the time and duration intervals in the partition to vary with the number of observations. Imposing no structural assumptions on the true intensities, we obtain asymptotic normality of the occurence/exposure rates under appropriate shrinking conditions on the partition lengths. We derive asymptotic normality results for both Markov and semi-Markov models using only classical central limit theorems and elementary results for counting processes. All results are illustrated on both simulated and real data.

</details>


### [142] [Improved estimation of positive powers of scale parameters of exponential distributions under a prior information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03754)
*Somnath Mondal*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了在尺度参数受先验排序约束条件下，两参数平移指数分布族中尺度参数正幂的估计问题，推导了等变估计量优于其他估计量的充分条件，提出了多种优于最佳仿射等变估计量（BAEE）的估计方法，包括光滑估计量（同时被证明是非信息先验下的广义贝叶斯估计量）和基于Pitman接近准则的改进估计量，并通过模拟和实例验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可靠性分析、生存研究和工程等领域中，参数估计常面临先验结构信息（如排序约束）的利用问题，忽略这些信息会导致估计不准确。本文旨在将先验排序约束纳入两参数平移指数分布的尺度参数正幂估计中，以提升估计效果。

Method: 推导等变估计量在尺度不变严格凸损失函数下占优的充分条件；构造多种优于BAEE的估计量；采用积分方法推导光滑估计量并证明其为非信息先验下的广义贝叶斯估计量；基于Pitman接近准则提出改进估计量；通过大规模模拟和实际数据实例进行验证。

Result: 成功推导了等变估计量的占优条件；获得了多种优于BAEE的估计量；光滑估计量被证明是广义贝叶斯估计量；基于Pitman准则的改进估计量表现出优势；模拟和实例结果支持了理论结论。

Conclusion: 在尺度参数排序约束下，利用先验信息可以显著改进两参数平移指数分布中尺度参数正幂的估计效果，所提出的多种估计方法均优于传统BAEE，为实际应用提供了有效工具。
